In `src/budgetGuard.ts`, add the SQL constants and replace `checkCaps` and `executionUsage`:

```ts
/** Spec §5.3 execution scopes: rows of this execution for the given providers, billed or free rule. */
export const EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE execution_id = @executionId
  AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@providers))
  AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (@billed = 0 OR outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)`;

/** The execution's own caps live in its frozen snapshot (spec §7 `budget_json`, §8.1 "its own execution caps"). */
const EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL = `
SELECT is_child AS isChild,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCalls') AS maxCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxFreeCalls') AS maxFreeCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCostMicroGbp') AS maxCostMicroGbp
FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?`;

interface ExecutionCaps { maxCalls: number; maxFreeCalls: number; maxCostMicroGbp: number }
```

```ts
  protected checkCaps(p: ReserveParams, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime): void {
    const billed = budget.billing === 'http-200';
    // 1. provider month or lifetime (calls)
    const prov = this.providerUsage(p.providerId, budget, now);
    if (budget.callCap !== null && prov.calls + 1 > budget.callCap) {
      throw this.exceeded(budget.window === 'lifetime' ? 'provider-lifetime' : 'provider-month', budget.callCap, prov.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
    // 2. global month (billed providers only; the fixed fees are inside g.cost)
    if (billed) {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      const cap = this.config.global.monthlyCapMicroGbp;
      // why: spec §5.3 "A cap of 0 refuses when current + this > 0; zero-cost calls pass."
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && g.cost + p.costMicroGbp > cap) throw this.exceeded('global-month', cap, g.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    }
    // 3. execution scopes, only for calls made on behalf of an execution
    if (p.executionId === null) return;
    const caps = this.executionCaps(p.executionId);
    if (billed) {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.billedProviders(), true);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-calls', caps.maxCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && u.cost + p.costMicroGbp > caps.maxCostMicroGbp) {
        throw this.exceeded('execution-cost', caps.maxCostMicroGbp, u.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
      }
    } else {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.freeProviders(), false);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxFreeCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-free-calls', caps.maxFreeCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
  }

  protected executionUsage(kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost', executionId: string): Usage {
    const caps = this.executionCaps(executionId);
    const billed = kind !== 'execution-free-calls';
    const u = this.executionRows(executionId, billed ? this.billedProviders() : this.freeProviders(), billed);
    const cap = kind === 'execution-calls' ? caps.maxCalls : kind === 'execution-free-calls' ? caps.maxFreeCalls : caps.maxCostMicroGbp;
    return { calls: u.calls, costMicroGbp: u.cost, cap, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null };
  }

  private executionRows(executionId: string, providers: LedgerProviderId[], billed: boolean): CallsAndCost {
    const row = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL)
      .get({ executionId, providers: JSON.stringify(providers), billed: billed ? 1 : 0 });
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: row?.cost ?? 0 };
  }

  private executionCaps(executionId: string): ExecutionCaps {
    const row = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { isChild: number; maxCalls: number | null; maxFreeCalls: number | null; maxCostMicroGbp: number | null }>(EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL)
      .get(executionId);
    if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} does not exist; refusing to reserve`);
    if (row.maxCalls === null || row.maxFreeCalls === null || row.maxCostMicroGbp === null) {
      throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} snapshot has no budget; refusing to reserve`);
    }
    // why: spec §8.1 a child has its own call cap, CHILD_MAX_CALLS; the stricter of the two applies.
    const maxCalls = row.isChild === 1 ? Math.min(row.maxCalls, this.config.childMaxCalls) : row.maxCalls;
    return { maxCalls, maxFreeCalls: row.maxFreeCalls, maxCostMicroGbp: row.maxCostMicroGbp };
  }
```

Check order `// why:` comment above `checkCaps`: provider first (a vendor's hard stop), then global (total spend), then the execution's own caps; one breach is reported, and the engine's error table (spec §8.1) acts on its scope.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Append to `packages/db/src/budgetConfig.ts` (and change its first import to `import { type Config, type ProviderId, type ProviderLimits, syntheticAllowed } from '@gmt/core';`):

```ts
function fromLimits(limits: ProviderLimits, billing: ProviderBudget['billing'], lifetimeOffset: number): ProviderBudget {
  return { billing, window: limits.window, billingDay: limits.billingDay, callCap: limits.monthlyCallCap, planFixedMicroGbp: limits.planFixedMicroGbp, lifetimeOffset };
}

/**
 * Builds the guard's configuration from the parsed config (spec §12). Only configured providers
 * appear; reserving for an absent provider throws (fail closed). The fixture provider appears only
 * under `syntheticAllowed` and only when the caller names its call cap (spec §5.3 "fixture iff HTTP
 * 200 at the configured test cost (only under syntheticAllowed)").
 */
export function budgetConfigFromConfig(config: Config, opts: { fixtureCallCap?: number }): BudgetConfig {
  const providers: BudgetConfig['providers'] = {
    // why: spec §5.3 "fx (never billable, no caps, its own host allowlist)"; ledgered so egress is visible.
    fx: { billing: 'never', window: 'month', billingDay: 1, callCap: null, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, lifetimeOffset: 0 },
  };
  const tp = config.providers.travelpayouts;
  if (tp !== null) providers.travelpayouts = fromLimits(tp.limits, 'never', 0);
  const sa = config.providers.searchapi;
  if (sa !== null) providers.searchapi = fromLimits(sa.limits, 'http-200', sa.limits.window === 'lifetime' ? sa.trialUsed : 0);
  if (opts.fixtureCallCap !== undefined && syntheticAllowed(config)) {
    providers.fixture = { billing: 'http-200', window: 'month', billingDay: 1, callCap: opts.fixtureCallCap, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, lifetimeOffset: 0 };
  }
  return { providers, global: { monthlyCapMicroGbp: config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp, billingDay: config.spend.globalBillingDay }, childMaxCalls: config.spend.childMaxCalls };
}
```

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { LedgerRepo } from '../src/repositories/ledger.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { rawExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const cfg = {
  providers: {
    searchapi: { billing: 'http-200' as const, window: 'month' as const, billingDay: 1, callCap: 10_000, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, lifetimeOffset: 0 },
    travelpayouts: { billing: 'never' as const, window: 'month' as const, billingDay: 1, callCap: 50_000, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, lifetimeOffset: 0 },
  },
  global: { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000, billingDay: 1 },
  childMaxCalls: 6,
};

describe('LedgerRepo (spec §5.3, §7, §8.1 parity)', () => {
  it('sweepPending: pending rows older than the cutoff become unknown and stay counted', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:00, left pending
    clock.advance(9 * 60_000);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:09, left pending
    clock.advance(2 * 60_000); // 12:11: boot
    const repo = new LedgerRepo(db, clock);
    expect(repo.sweepPending('2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime)).toBe(1); // boot − 10 min
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT outcome FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ outcome: 'unknown' }, { outcome: 'pending' }]);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 2, costMicroGbp: 6400 });
  });

  it('countsFor: calls exclude cache hits; tiles = distinct (provider, tile) over first attempts and cache hits', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 100_000 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, fixedClock(T0));
    const call = (tileIndex: number, httpAttempt: number) =>
      guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt, endpointPath: '/p', tileIndex }).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 });
    call(0, 1); call(0, 1); // tile 0, two pages
    call(1, 1); call(1, 2); // tile 1, a retry
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, endpointPath: '/p', tileIndex: 2 });
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 });
    expect(new LedgerRepo(db, fixedClock(T0)).countsFor(executionId)).toEqual({ calls: 5, costMicroGbp: 3200, tiles: 3, cacheHits: 1 });
  });

  it('overrun: actual calls more than 10% above the pre-factor plan length', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, fixedClock(T0));
    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i++) guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 });
    const repo = new LedgerRepo(db, fixedClock(T0));
    expect(repo.overrun(executionId, 10)).toBe(false); // 11 = 10 × 1.1, not more
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 });
    expect(repo.overrun(executionId, 10)).toBe(true); // 12 > 11
  });

  it('pruneCacheHits removes only cache_hit rows older than the cutoff for the named providers', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock('2026-06-01T00:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime);
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, endpointPath: '/p' });
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, endpointPath: '/s' });
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 });
    const repo = new LedgerRepo(db, clock);
    expect(repo.pruneCacheHits(T0, ['travelpayouts'])).toBe(1);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT provider_id AS p, outcome AS o FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([
      { p: 'searchapi', o: 'cache_hit' },
      { p: 'travelpayouts', o: 'ok' },
    ]);
    expect(repo.pruneCacheHits(T0, [])).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL, `Cannot find module '../src/repositories/ledger.js'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime } from '@gmt/core';
import type { LedgerProviderId } from '../budgetConfig.js';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { assertInt, assertIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';

/**
 * Reads and housekeeping over `provider_requests` (spec §7). Inserts and finalisation belong to
 * `SqliteBudgetGuard`; nothing else writes the ledger except the boot sweep and retention here.
 */
export class LedgerRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: held for the uniform repository constructor (plan contract `new XRepo(db, clock)`); cutoffs are passed in.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Actuals for one execution (spec §8.1 "Actuals come from the ledger by execution_id").
   * `tiles` = distinct (provider, tile_index) among first-attempt rows and cache hits: the parity count.
   */
  countsFor(executionId: string): { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number } {
    const row = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { calls: number; cost: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }>(
        `SELECT
           SUM(outcome <> 'cache_hit') AS calls,
           COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost,
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN tile_index IS NOT NULL AND (http_attempt = 1 OR outcome = 'cache_hit') THEN provider_id || ':' || tile_index END) AS tiles,
           SUM(outcome = 'cache_hit') AS cacheHits
         FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ?`,
      )
      .get(executionId);
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, costMicroGbp: row?.cost ?? 0, tiles: row?.tiles ?? 0, cacheHits: row?.cacheHits ?? 0 };
  }

  /** Spec §8.1: actual calls exceed the pre-factor plan by more than 10% (integer arithmetic: calls × 10 > plan × 11). */
  overrun(executionId: string, planLength: number): boolean {
    assertInt('planLength', planLength);
    return this.countsFor(executionId).calls * 10 > planLength * 11;
  }

  /** Spec §5.3 "boot sweep marks pending older than 10 minutes unknown (still counted)". The caller passes boot − 10 min. */
  sweepPending(olderThan: IsoDateTime): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    return this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE provider_requests SET outcome = 'unknown' WHERE outcome = 'pending' AND started_at < ?`).run(olderThan).changes;
  }

  /** Spec §7 retention: prune `cache_hit` rows older than the cutoff, only for the named (monthly-window) providers. */
  pruneCacheHits(olderThan: IsoDateTime, providerIds: readonly LedgerProviderId[]): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    if (providerIds.length === 0) return 0;
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare(`DELETE FROM provider_requests WHERE outcome = 'cache_hit' AND started_at < @olderThan AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@ids))`)
      .run({ olderThan, ids: JSON.stringify(providerIds) }).changes;
  }

}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { openDatabase } from '../src/client.js';
import { applyMigrations, pendingMigrations } from '../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../src/paths.js';
import { tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';

const schemaOf = (db: ReturnType<typeof openDatabase>) =>
  db.raw.prepare(`SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY type, name`).all();

describe('migrations (spec §7 "Migrations", §9 item 3)', () => {
  it('apply twice: the second run applies nothing and changes nothing', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(tmpDbPath());
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual(['0000_initial']);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: ['0000_initial'] });
    const before = schemaOf(db);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: [] });
    expect(schemaOf(db)).toEqual(before);
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual([]);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM __drizzle_migrations').get()).toEqual({ n: 1 });
    db.close();
  });

  it('a missing migrations directory fails naming the directory', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(':memory:');
    expect(() => applyMigrations(db, '/nonexistent/drizzle')).toThrow(/\/nonexistent\/drizzle/);
    db.close();
  });
});

describe('transactions (spec §7 "Transactions")', () => {
  it('an async callback is rejected and its synchronous writes are rolled back', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(':memory:');
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const insert = () =>
      db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('fx', '/v1/latest', '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 'pending', 0, 1)`).run();
    // @ts-expect-error repository transaction callbacks are typed to return non-Promise values
    expect(() => db.transaction(async () => insert())).toThrow(/synchronous/);
    // why: better-sqlite3 runs the callback up to its first await, so the insert happened; the rollback must undo it.
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM provider_requests').get()).toEqual({ n: 0 });
    db.close();
  });
});

describe('SQLite settings on a file database (spec §7, plan Review Focus 5)', () => {
  it('busy_timeout=5000, WAL and foreign keys hold on a real file', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openDatabase(path);
    expect(db.raw.pragma('busy_timeout', { simple: true })).toBe(5000);
    expect(db.raw.pragma('journal_mode', { simple: true })).toBe('wal');
    expect(db.raw.pragma('foreign_keys', { simple: true })).toBe(1);
    // a second handle on the same file gets the same settings (the tool bootstrap opens its own handle)
    const second = openDatabase(path);
    expect(second.raw.pragma('busy_timeout', { simple: true })).toBe(5000);
    second.close();
    db.close();
  });

  it('a write blocked by another connection surfaces SqliteError SQLITE_BUSY, not a hang or a crash', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openDatabase(path);
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const other = openDatabase(path);
    other.raw.pragma('busy_timeout = 50'); // why: keep the test fast; production uses 5000 (asserted above)
    db.raw.prepare('BEGIN IMMEDIATE').run();
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      other.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('fx', '/v1', '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 'pending', 0, 1)`).run();
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    db.raw.prepare('ROLLBACK').run();
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    expect((thrown as InstanceType<typeof Database.SqliteError>).code).toBe('SQLITE_BUSY');
    other.close();
    db.close();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Replace `packages/db/src/migrate.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { migrate } from 'drizzle-orm/better-sqlite3/migrator';
import type { Db } from './client.js';

interface JournalEntry { idx: number; tag: string; when: number }

/** drizzle-kit 0.31 layout: `<dir>/meta/_journal.json` lists every migration with its `when` stamp. */
function journal(migrationsDir: string): JournalEntry[] {
  const file = join(migrationsDir, 'meta', '_journal.json');
  if (!existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`no migration journal in ${migrationsDir}`);
  return (JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')) as { entries: JournalEntry[] }).entries;
}

/** `created_at` stamps the Drizzle migrator recorded (it stores each journal entry's `when`). */
function appliedStamps(db: Db): Set<number> {
  const table = db.raw.prepare(`SELECT 1 AS present FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = '__drizzle_migrations'`).get();
  if (table === undefined) return new Set();
  const rows = db.raw.prepare<[], { c: number | string }>('SELECT created_at AS c FROM __drizzle_migrations').all();
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => Number(r.c)));
}

/** Tags of migrations not yet applied. WP8 takes the `VACUUM INTO` backup only when this is non-empty (spec §7). */
export function pendingMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): string[] {
  const done = appliedStamps(db);
  return journal(migrationsDir).filter((e) => !done.has(e.when)).map((e) => e.tag);
}

/** Applies the committed Drizzle SQL migrations idempotently and reports which ran. Spec §7 "Migrations". */
export function applyMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): { applied: string[] } {
  const pending = pendingMigrations(db, migrationsDir);
  if (pending.length === 0) return { applied: [] };
  try {
    migrate(db.drizzle, { migrationsFolder: migrationsDir });
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(`migration failed in ${migrationsDir} (pending: ${pending.join(', ')}): ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  }
  return { applied: pending };
}
```

```ts
const A = 'A'.charCodeAt(0);
/** Synthetic IATA-shaped codes AAA, AAB, …; clearly not real airports' rows (names say so). */
function syntheticCode(i: number): string {
  return String.fromCharCode(A + Math.floor(i / 676), A + (Math.floor(i / 26) % 26), A + (i % 26));
}
function syntheticDataset(n: number, version = 'synthetic-1', metro: (i: number) => string | null = () => null): AirportsDataset {

}

  });

    expect(seedAirports(db, syntheticDataset(3, 'v1'))).toEqual({ inserted: 0, datasetVersion: 'v1' });

    expect(repo.get('EWR')).toMatchObject({ tz: 'America/New_York' });

describe('AirportsRepo', () => {

  });

    expect(repo.exists('AAA')).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.exists('ZZZ')).toBe(false);

    expect(repo.metroOf('CCC')).toBeNull();

    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('CCC')])).toBeNull(); // one has none
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('ZZZ')])).toBeNull(); // one is unknown
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([])).toBeNull();

    const repo = repoWith([row('AAB', { name: 'Alpha Field' }), row('AAA', { name: 'Beta Field' }), row('BAA', { name: 'Field AA' }), row('CCC', { name: '100% Synthetic' })]);
    expect(repo.search('aaa', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA']); // case-insensitive exact code
    expect(repo.search('AA', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA', 'AAB', 'BAA']);
    expect(repo.search('alpha', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAB']);
    expect(repo.search('%', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['CCC']);
    expect(repo.search('  ', 10)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.search('field', 1)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('rejects malformed IATA codes and unknown time zones', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const repo = new AirportsRepo(db, fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll([row('aa1')], 'v', 's')).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll([row('AAA', { tz: 'Mars/Olympus_Mons' })], 'v', 's')).toThrow(/tz/);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll([row('AAA', { metro: 'x' })], 'v', 's')).toThrow(/metro/);
    expect(repo.count()).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, real, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/**
 * Spec §7 `airports`, seeded from the vendored mwgg/Airports dataset. `tz` is the IANA zone every
 * local departure time is derived in (spec §5.6); `metro` is the IATA metropolitan code when the
 * dataset provides one (it drives Travelpayouts metro tiling, spec §5.2).
 */
export const airports = sqliteTable(
  'airports',
  {
    iata: text('iata').primaryKey(),
    icao: text('icao'),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    city: text('city'),
    country: text('country').notNull(),
    tz: text('tz').notNull(),
    lat: real('lat'),
    lon: real('lon'),
    metro: text('metro'),
    source: text('source').notNull(),
    datasetVersion: text('dataset_version').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('airports_iata_ck', sql.raw("iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_metro_ck', sql.raw("metro IS NULL OR metro GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_tz_ck', sql.raw('length(tz) > 0')),
    index('airports_metro_idx').on(t.metro),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/airports.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, Iata } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { Airport, AirportInput } from '../types.js';

interface AirportDbRow { iata: string; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string | null; country: string; tz: string; lat: number | null; lon: number | null; metro: string | null; source: string; dataset_version: string }

function toAirport(r: AirportDbRow): Airport {
  return { iata: r.iata as Iata, icao: r.icao, name: r.name, city: r.city, country: r.country, tz: r.tz, lat: r.lat, lon: r.lon, metro: r.metro, source: r.source, datasetVersion: r.dataset_version };
}

const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;

/** True when `tz` is a zone this runtime's Intl knows (normalisation depends on it, spec §5.6). */
function isKnownTimeZone(tz: string): boolean {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Airport lookups for validation, tiling and normalisation (spec §5.2, §5.6, §6.1). No airport is
 * named in code: every answer comes from the seeded table.
 */
export class AirportsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); airports carry no timestamps.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  exists(iata: string): boolean {
    return this.db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata) !== undefined;
  }

  get(iata: string): Airport | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], AirportDbRow>('SELECT * FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata);
    return r === undefined ? null : toAirport(r);
  }

  /** Autocomplete for the Definitions form (spec §11): exact code, then code prefix, then name/city substring. */
  search(q: string, limit: number): Airport[] {
    const needle = q.trim();
    if (needle.length === 0) return [];
    // why: the operator's text is matched literally; % and _ are escaped for LIKE.
    const escaped = needle.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`);
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, AirportDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM airports
         WHERE iata = upper(@q) OR iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
            OR name LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\' OR city LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
         ORDER BY CASE WHEN iata = upper(@q) THEN 0 WHEN iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, iata
         LIMIT @limit`,
      )
      .all({ q: needle, e: escaped, limit: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(limit)) })
      .map(toAirport);
  }

  metroOf(iata: string): string | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { metro: string | null }>('SELECT metro FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata);
    return r?.metro ?? null;
  }

  /** Every airport the table maps to a metro code (WP1's validation names them, plan Review Focus 1). */
  airportsOfMetro(code: string): Iata[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { iata: string }>('SELECT iata FROM airports WHERE metro = ? ORDER BY iata').all(code).map((r) => r.iata as Iata);
  }

  /** The one metro code every given airport shares, else null (spec §5.2 Travelpayouts metro tiling). */
  sharedMetro(iatas: readonly Iata[]): string | null {
    const unique = [...new Set(iatas)];
    if (unique.length === 0) return null;
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { n: number; nulls: number; distinctMetros: number; metro: string | null }>(
        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, SUM(metro IS NULL) AS nulls, COUNT(DISTINCT metro) AS distinctMetros, MIN(metro) AS metro
         FROM airports WHERE iata IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?))`,
      )
      .get(JSON.stringify(unique));
    if (r === undefined || r.n !== unique.length || r.nulls > 0 || r.distinctMetros !== 1) return null;
    return r.metro;
  }

  count(): number {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports').get()?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** Upsert by IATA in one transaction; every row is validated first so a bad dataset writes nothing. */
  upsertAll(rows: readonly AirportInput[], datasetVersion: string, source: string): void {
    for (const r of rows) {
      if (!IATA.test(r.iata)) throw new RowRejected('iata', `airport code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${r.iata}`);
      if (r.metro !== null && !IATA.test(r.metro)) throw new RowRejected('metro', `metro code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${r.metro} for ${r.iata}`);
      if (!isKnownTimeZone(r.tz)) throw new RowRejected('tz', `unknown time zone ${r.tz} for ${r.iata}`);
    }
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO airports (iata, icao, name, city, country, tz, lat, lon, metro, source, dataset_version)
       VALUES (@iata, @icao, @name, @city, @country, @tz, @lat, @lon, @metro, @source, @datasetVersion)
       ON CONFLICT(iata) DO UPDATE SET icao = excluded.icao, name = excluded.name, city = excluded.city, country = excluded.country, tz = excluded.tz,
         lat = excluded.lat, lon = excluded.lon, metro = excluded.metro, source = excluded.source, dataset_version = excluded.dataset_version`,
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) stmt.run({ ...r, source, datasetVersion });
    });
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/seed/airports.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { type Iata, systemClock } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { AirportsRepo } from '../repositories/airports.js';
import type { AirportsDataset } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7: boot fails unless at least this many airports carry an IATA code and a tz. */
export const AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS = 5000;

/** Reads the vendored dataset from disk. No network is involved anywhere in seeding (spec §7). */
export function loadAirportsDataset(path: string): AirportsDataset {
  const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, 'utf8')) as Partial<AirportsDataset>;
  if (typeof parsed.version !== 'string' || typeof parsed.source !== 'string' || typeof parsed.licence !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(parsed.airports)) {
    throw new Error(`airports dataset ${path} lacks version, source, licence or airports`);
  }
  return parsed as AirportsDataset;
}

/**
 * Seeds or re-seeds `airports` (spec §7 "re-seeded by upsert when the dataset version changes").
 * Skips when every dataset row is already present at this version.
 */
export function seedAirports(db: Db, dataset: AirportsDataset): { inserted: number; datasetVersion: string } {
  const atVersion = db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE dataset_version = ?').get(dataset.version)?.n ?? 0;
  if (atVersion >= dataset.airports.length && atVersion > 0) return { inserted: 0, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
  // why: AirportsRepo owns validation and the upsert; airports carry no timestamps, so the system clock is never read.
  const repo = new AirportsRepo(db, systemClock);
  repo.upsertAll(dataset.airports, dataset.version, dataset.source);
  return { inserted: dataset.airports.length, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
}

/**
 * Spec §7 sanity check, run at boot after seeding: ≥ 5,000 rows with IATA and tz, and every airport
 * referenced by an enabled definition present. A failure fails boot (WP8 refuses to serve).
 */
export function sanityCheckAirports(db: Db, referenced: readonly Iata[]): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  const n = db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND length(tz) > 0`).get()?.n ?? 0;
  if (n < AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `airports table holds ${n} rows with an IATA code and tz; at least ${AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS} are required` };
  }
  const exists = db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?');
  const missing = [...new Set(referenced)].filter((iata) => exists.get(iata) === undefined).sort();
  if (missing.length > 0) return { ok: false, reason: `airports referenced by enabled definitions are missing: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Builds packages/db/data/airports.<date>-<sha7>.json from a downloaded mwgg/Airports airports.json.
// Spec §7: the dataset is vendored and versioned; the app never downloads anything at seed.
// Run by a person when updating the dataset, never by the app or CI.
//
// Usage:
//   node packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs --input <airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD> [--metro <file>]
// --metro is optional: a JSON array of { code, city_code, iata_type } rows (see the brief's open question);
// without it every airport's metro is null and Travelpayouts tiles per airport (spec §5.2 "or 28 by airport").
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

const MIN_ROWS = 5000; // spec §7 sanity threshold; the build refuses to write a smaller file
const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;

const { values } = parseArgs({ options: { input: { type: 'string' }, commit: { type: 'string' }, date: { type: 'string' }, metro: { type: 'string' } } });
if (!values.input || !/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(values.commit ?? '') || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(values.date ?? '')) {
  console.error('usage: build-airports.mjs --input <airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD> [--metro <file>]');
  process.exit(2);
}

const metroByIata = new Map();
if (values.metro) {
  for (const a of JSON.parse(readFileSync(values.metro, 'utf8'))) {
    // why: a metro code is only meaningful when it differs from the airport's own code (e.g. LGW → LON).
    if (a.iata_type === 'airport' && IATA.test(a.code ?? '') && IATA.test(a.city_code ?? '') && a.city_code !== a.code) metroByIata.set(a.code, a.city_code);
  }
}

const knownTz = (tz) => {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(values.input, 'utf8'));
const seen = new Set();
const airports = [];
let skippedTz = 0;
for (const a of Object.values(raw)) {
  const iata = typeof a.iata === 'string' ? a.iata.trim() : '';
  if (!IATA.test(iata) || typeof a.tz !== 'string' || a.tz.length === 0) continue;
  if (!knownTz(a.tz)) {
    skippedTz++;
    continue;
  }
  if (seen.has(iata)) {
    console.error(`duplicate IATA code ${iata}; refusing to guess which row is right`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  seen.add(iata);
  airports.push({
    iata,
    icao: typeof a.icao === 'string' && a.icao.length > 0 ? a.icao : null,
    name: String(a.name),
    city: typeof a.city === 'string' && a.city.length > 0 ? a.city : null,
    country: String(a.country),
    tz: a.tz,
    lat: typeof a.lat === 'number' ? a.lat : null,
    lon: typeof a.lon === 'number' ? a.lon : null,
    metro: metroByIata.get(iata) ?? null,
  });
}
airports.sort((x, y) => (x.iata < y.iata ? -1 : x.iata > y.iata ? 1 : 0));
if (airports.length < MIN_ROWS) {
  console.error(`only ${airports.length} airports with IATA and tz; at least ${MIN_ROWS} required`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const version = `${values.date}-${values.commit.slice(0, 7)}`;
const source = `mwgg/Airports@${values.commit}${values.metro ? ` + metro codes from ${basename(values.metro)}` : ''}`;
const out = fileURLToPath(new URL(`../data/airports.${version}.json`, import.meta.url));
writeFileSync(out, `${JSON.stringify({ version, source, licence: 'MIT; see airports.LICENSE', airports })}\n`);
console.log(`wrote ${airports.length} airports (${metroByIata.size} metro mappings, ${skippedTz} unknown time zones skipped), version ${version}`);
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isInteger } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `fx_rates`: GBP per 1 native unit × 1e9, per ECB date (spec §6.3). */
export const fxRates = sqliteTable(
  'fx_rates',
  {
    date: text('date').notNull(),
    currency: text('currency').notNull(),
    rateNano: integer('rate_nano').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.date, t.currency] }),
    check('fx_rates_rate_ck', sql.raw("typeof(rate_nano) = 'integer' AND rate_nano > 0")),
    check('fx_rates_currency_ck', sql.raw("currency GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND currency <> 'GBP'")),
    check('fx_rates_date_ck', sql.raw("date GLOB '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]'")),
    check('fx_rates_int_ck', isInteger('rate_nano')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/fx.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { FxRateRow } from '../types.js';

/** FX rates for normalisation (spec §5.6: `FX_MAX_RATE_AGE_DAYS`, ECB-covered currencies only). */
export class FxRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); `today` is passed explicitly by callers.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  rateNano(date: IsoDate, currency: string): number | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string, string], { r: number }>('SELECT rate_nano AS r FROM fx_rates WHERE date = ? AND currency = ?').get(date, currency)?.r ?? null;
  }

  /** The newest rate dated within [today − maxAgeDays, today]; null means `fx-unavailable` (spec §5.6). */
  latest(currency: string, maxAgeDays: number, today: IsoDate): { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate } | null {
    assertInt('maxAgeDays', maxAgeDays);
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate }>(
        `SELECT rate_nano AS rateNano, date FROM fx_rates
         WHERE currency = @currency AND date <= @today AND date >= date(@today, '-' || @maxAgeDays || ' days')
         ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ currency, today, maxAgeDays });
    return r ?? null;
  }

  upsert(rows: readonly FxRateRow[]): void {
    for (const r of rows) {
      assertIsoDate('date', r.date);
      if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(r.currency)) throw new RowRejected('currency', `currency must be ISO 4217 upper-case, got ${r.currency}`);
      if (r.currency === 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('currency', 'GBP is the base currency and has no rate row');
      assertInt('rate_nano', r.rateNano, 1);
    }
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO fx_rates (date, currency, rate_nano) VALUES (@date, @currency, @rateNano) ON CONFLICT(date, currency) DO UPDATE SET rate_nano = excluded.rate_nano',
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) stmt.run(r);
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { DefinitionInput } from '../../src/types.js';

/** The worked example definition (spec §1, §11 defaults): LHR, LGW → JFK, EWR, 10–21 nights, 182-day relative window. */
export function exampleDefinitionInput(over: Partial<DefinitionInput> = {}): DefinitionInput {
  return {
    name: 'London to New York',
    origins: [LHR, LGW],
    destinations: [JFK, EWR],
    window: { kind: 'relative', startOffsetDays: 7, lengthDays: 182 },
    minNights: 10,
    maxNights: 21,
    cabin: 'economy',
    constraints: { maxJourneyMinutes: 1500, arrivalWindowLocal: { from: '06:00', to: '23:00' } },
    verification: { maxCells: 10, topN: 10, alwaysInclude: [], expandTop: 2, bookingLinks: true },
    budget: { maxCalls: 2000, maxFreeCalls: 2000, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 },
    providers: { discovery: ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi'], verification: ['searchapi'], discoveryRefreshHours: { travelpayouts: 168, searchapi: 168 } },
    destinationNotes: {},
    onwardCostPence: {},
    scheduleCron: '0 5 * * 1',
    scheduleTz: 'Europe/London',
    confirmedCostPence: null,
    enabled: true,
    ...over,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { JFK, exampleDefinitionInput } from './helpers/factories.js';

describe('DefinitionsRepo (spec §7 search_definitions)', () => {
  it('round-trips every field', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    const created = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ destinationNotes: { [JFK]: 'onward leg not included' }, onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 4500 } }));
    expect(created).toEqual({ ...exampleDefinitionInput({ destinationNotes: { [JFK]: 'onward leg not included' }, onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 4500 } }), id: created.id, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: T0, deletedAt: null });
    expect(repo.get(created.id)).toEqual(created);
    expect(repo.get('missing')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('update merges a patch and moves updated_at', () => {
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), clock);
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const u = repo.update(d.id, { maxNights: 14, enabled: false });
    expect(u).toMatchObject({ maxNights: 14, enabled: false, minNights: 10, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: '2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z' });
  });

  it('soft delete: hidden from list, kept for history, refuses updates, keeps executions', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock);
    const keep = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'kept' }));
    const gone = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'gone' }));
    db.raw
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, definition_snapshot_json, status, started_at) VALUES ('e1', ?, '{}', 'completed', ?)`)
      .run(gone.id, T0);
    clock.advance(1000);
    repo.softDelete(gone.id);
    expect(repo.list().map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(['kept']);
    expect(repo.get(gone.id)).toMatchObject({ deletedAt: '2026-10-14T12:00:01.000Z', enabled: false });
    expect(() => repo.update(gone.id, { name: 'x' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.softDelete('missing')).toThrow(/missing/);
    expect(db.raw.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ?`).get(gone.id)).toEqual({ n: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(keep.id)?.deletedAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setConfirmedCost stores integer pence only', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 412);
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBe(412);
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, null);
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBeNull();
    expect(() => repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 4.5)).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('rejects non-integer nights, budgets and onward costs', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ minNights: 10.5 }))).toThrow(/min_nights/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ budget: { maxCalls: 1, maxFreeCalls: 1, maxCostMicroGbp: 0.5 } }))).toThrow(/maxCostMicroGbp/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 12.3 } }))).toThrow(/onward/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertNullableInt } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { DefinitionInput } from '../types.js';

interface DefinitionDbRow {
  id: string; name: string; origins_json: string; destinations_json: string; window_json: string; min_nights: number; max_nights: number;
  cabin: SearchDefinition['cabin']; constraints_json: string; verification_policy_json: string; budget_json: string; providers_json: string;
  destination_notes_json: string; onward_cost_json: string | null; schedule_cron: string | null; schedule_tz: string;
  confirmed_cost_pence: number | null; enabled: number; deleted_at: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
}

function toDefinition(r: DefinitionDbRow): SearchDefinition {
  return {
    id: r.id, name: r.name,
    origins: JSON.parse(r.origins_json), destinations: JSON.parse(r.destinations_json), window: JSON.parse(r.window_json),
    minNights: r.min_nights, maxNights: r.max_nights, cabin: r.cabin,
    constraints: JSON.parse(r.constraints_json), verification: JSON.parse(r.verification_policy_json), budget: JSON.parse(r.budget_json),
    providers: JSON.parse(r.providers_json), destinationNotes: JSON.parse(r.destination_notes_json),
    onwardCostPence: r.onward_cost_json === null ? {} : JSON.parse(r.onward_cost_json),
    scheduleCron: r.schedule_cron, scheduleTz: r.schedule_tz, confirmedCostPence: r.confirmed_cost_pence, enabled: r.enabled === 1,
    createdAt: r.created_at as IsoDateTime, updatedAt: r.updated_at as IsoDateTime, deletedAt: r.deleted_at as IsoDateTime | null,
  };
}

/** Integer rules the repository owns (spec §6.3); shape and range rules are zod's in `api-contract`. */
function validate(d: DefinitionInput): void {
  assertInt('min_nights', d.minNights);
  assertInt('max_nights', d.maxNights);
  assertInt('budget.maxCalls', d.budget.maxCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxFreeCalls', d.budget.maxFreeCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxCostMicroGbp', d.budget.maxCostMicroGbp);
  assertNullableInt('confirmed_cost_pence', d.confirmedCostPence);
  for (const [dest, pence] of Object.entries(d.onwardCostPence)) if (pence !== undefined) assertInt(`onward cost for ${dest}`, pence);
  if (d.scheduleTz.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('schedule-tz', 'schedule_tz is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
}

const COLUMNS = `name = @name, origins_json = @origins, destinations_json = @destinations, window_json = @window, min_nights = @minNights, max_nights = @maxNights,
  cabin = @cabin, constraints_json = @constraints, verification_policy_json = @verification, budget_json = @budget, providers_json = @providers,
  destination_notes_json = @destinationNotes, onward_cost_json = @onwardCost, schedule_cron = @scheduleCron, schedule_tz = @scheduleTz,
  confirmed_cost_pence = @confirmedCostPence, enabled = @enabled, updated_at = @updatedAt`;

function params(d: DefinitionInput, updatedAt: IsoDateTime): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    name: d.name, origins: JSON.stringify(d.origins), destinations: JSON.stringify(d.destinations), window: JSON.stringify(d.window),
    minNights: d.minNights, maxNights: d.maxNights, cabin: d.cabin, constraints: JSON.stringify(d.constraints),
    verification: JSON.stringify(d.verification), budget: JSON.stringify(d.budget), providers: JSON.stringify(d.providers),
    destinationNotes: JSON.stringify(d.destinationNotes), onwardCost: JSON.stringify(d.onwardCostPence),
    scheduleCron: d.scheduleCron, scheduleTz: d.scheduleTz, confirmedCostPence: d.confirmedCostPence, enabled: d.enabled ? 1 : 0, updatedAt,
  };
}

/** Spec §7 `search_definitions`, soft-deleted. `confirmed_cost_pence` changes only through `setConfirmedCost`. */
export class DefinitionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  create(input: DefinitionInput): SearchDefinition {
    validate(input);
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_definitions (id, created_at, name, origins_json, destinations_json, window_json, min_nights, max_nights, cabin, constraints_json,
          verification_policy_json, budget_json, providers_json, destination_notes_json, onward_cost_json, schedule_cron, schedule_tz, confirmed_cost_pence, enabled, updated_at)
         VALUES (@id, @createdAt, @name, @origins, @destinations, @window, @minNights, @maxNights, @cabin, @constraints, @verification, @budget, @providers,
          @destinationNotes, @onwardCost, @scheduleCron, @scheduleTz, @confirmedCostPence, @enabled, @updatedAt)`,
      )
      .run({ id, createdAt: now, ...params(input, now) });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  update(id: string, patch: Partial<DefinitionInput>): SearchDefinition {
    const existing = this.mustGet(id);
    if (existing.deletedAt !== null) throw new RowRejected('deleted', `definition ${id} is deleted`);
    const { id: _id, createdAt: _c, updatedAt: _u, deletedAt: _d, ...current } = existing;
    const merged: DefinitionInput = { ...current, ...patch };
    validate(merged);
    this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_definitions SET ${COLUMNS} WHERE id = @id`).run({ id, ...params(merged, this.clock.now()) });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  /** Returns soft-deleted definitions too (history views need them); `list` hides them. */
  get(id: string): SearchDefinition | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : toDefinition(r);
  }

  list(): SearchDefinition[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at, id').all().map(toDefinition);
  }

  /** Spec §7 "Soft-deleted": executions, prices and history keep their foreign keys; the schedule is disabled. */
  softDelete(id: string): void {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const changes = this.db.raw
      .prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET deleted_at = @now, enabled = 0, updated_at = @now WHERE id = @id AND deleted_at IS NULL')
      .run({ id, now }).changes;
    if (changes === 0 && this.get(id) === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /** Spec §7: set only from an explicit operator confirmation, never recomputed automatically. */
  setConfirmedCost(id: string, pence: number | null): void {
    assertNullableInt('confirmed_cost_pence', pence);
    const changes = this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET confirmed_cost_pence = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(pence, this.clock.now(), id).changes;
    if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): SearchDefinition {
    const d = this.get(id);
    if (d === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
    return d;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Add to `test/helpers/factories.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, DefinitionSnapshot, ExecutionStatus, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { Execution, ExecutionCreateInput } from '../../src/types.js';

/** A frozen snapshot of the worked example resolved at T0 (spec §3 "snapshot"). */
export function snapshotOf(d: SearchDefinition): DefinitionSnapshot {
  return {
    ...d,
    resolved: { origins: d.origins, destinations: d.destinations, departureFrom: asIsoDate('2026-10-21'), departureTo: asIsoDate('2027-04-20'), minNights: d.minNights, maxNights: d.maxNights },
    resolvedAt: T0,
    windowClamped: false,
  };
}

const CREATABLE: ReadonlySet<ExecutionStatus> = new Set(['pending', 'running', 'skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed']);

/**
 * Creates (or reuses) a definition and one execution through the repositories. A status the
 * repository cannot create directly (e.g. `completed`) is reached by creating `running` and then
 * moving it with `setStatus`, the path the engine takes.
 */
export function newExecution(
  db: Db,
  clock: Clock,
  opts: { definition?: SearchDefinition; status?: ExecutionStatus; cell?: CellKey | null } = {},
): { definition: SearchDefinition; execution: Execution } {
  const definition = opts.definition ?? new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock).create(exampleDefinitionInput());
  const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
  const wanted = opts.status ?? 'running';
  const createAs = (CREATABLE.has(wanted) ? wanted : 'running') as ExecutionCreateInput['status'];
  const created = executions.create({ definitionId: definition.id, snapshot: snapshotOf(definition), status: createAs, cell: opts.cell ?? null, estimate: null, errorText: null });
  if (createAs === wanted) return { definition, execution: created };
  executions.setStatus(created.id, wanted, { finishedAt: clock.now() });
  const moved = executions.get(created.id);
  if (moved === null) throw new Error('execution vanished');
  return { definition, execution: moved };
}
```

`packages/db/test/helpers/claim-worker.mjs`:

```js
// One contender in the compare-and-set race (spec §8.1 "Resume is a compare-and-set").
// Opens its own connection, waits at a shared barrier so both contenders fire together, then runs
// the repository's own claim statement and reports `changes`.
import { parentPort, workerData } from 'node:worker_threads';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

const { path, sql, id, barrier } = workerData;
const db = new Database(path);
db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
const gate = new Int32Array(barrier);
Atomics.add(gate, 0, 1);
Atomics.notify(gate, 0);
while (Atomics.load(gate, 0) < 2) Atomics.wait(gate, 0, 1, 50);
const { changes } = db.prepare(sql).run(id);
db.close();
parentPort.postMessage(changes);
```

`packages/db/test/executions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import { ACTIVE_STATUSES, type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { openDatabase } from '../src/client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../src/schema/checks.js';
import { T0, freshDb, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution, snapshotOf } from './helpers/factories.js';

const isUniqueViolation = (e: unknown) => e instanceof Database.SqliteError && e.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE';

describe('ExecutionsRepo (spec §7 search_executions, §8.1)', () => {
  it('the schema active set matches core ACTIVE_STATUSES', () => {
    expect(ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL).toBe(ACTIVE_STATUSES.map((s) => `'${s}'`).join(', '));
  });

  it('round-trips a parent and a child; a skipped execution is created finished', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending' });
    expect(execution).toMatchObject({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: false, cell: null, status: 'pending', stage: null, autoResumeCount: 0, startedAt: T0, finishedAt: null, cancelRequested: false });
    expect(execution.snapshot).toEqual(snapshotOf(definition));
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'running', cell: cell() }).execution;
    expect(child).toMatchObject({ isChild: true, cell: cell() });
    const skipped = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'skipped_window' }).execution;
    expect(skipped).toMatchObject({ status: 'skipped_window', finishedAt: T0 });
  });

  it('partial unique index: one active parent per definition', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending' });
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'running' });
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(isUniqueViolation(thrown)).toBe(true);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed', { finishedAt: T0 });
    expect(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'running' })).not.toThrow();
    const sql = db.raw.prepare(`SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = 'search_executions_active_parent_uq'`).get() as { sql: string };
    expect(sql.sql).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 0 AND status IN \('pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'\)/);
  });

  it('partial unique index: one active child per (definition, cell); parent and child coexist', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { definition } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'running' });
    newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() });
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() });
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(isUniqueViolation(thrown)).toBe(true);
    expect(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) })).not.toThrow();
    const sql = db.raw.prepare(`SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = 'search_executions_active_child_uq'`).get() as { sql: string };
    expect(sql.sql).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 1 AND status IN/);
  });

  it('activeParent, activeChild, latestCompleted, listForDefinition', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: first } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.setStatus(first.id, 'completed');
    clock.advance(1000);
    const second = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution;
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }).execution;
    expect(repo.activeParent(definition.id)?.id).toBe(second.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell())?.id).toBe(child.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell(undefined, EWR))).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.latestCompleted(definition.id)?.id).toBe(first.id);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: false }).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([second.id, first.id]);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 1, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('setStatus applies only the patch keys given and strips URLs from error_text', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'running', { stage: 'discovery' });
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'paused_error', {
      resumeAfter: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime,
      errorText: 'SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://user:pw@api.example.com/v1/x?api_key=SECRET',
    });
    const e = repo.get(execution.id);
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_error', stage: 'discovery', resumeAfter: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(e?.errorText).toBe('SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://api.example.com/v1/x');
    expect(() => repo.setStatus('missing', 'running')).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'running', { resumeAfter: 'tomorrow' as IsoDateTime })).toThrow(/toISOString/);
  });

  it('claimResume is a compare-and-set: resumable → running once; non-resumable and conflicting claims lose', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'paused_budget', { resumeAfter: '2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime });
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'running', resumeAfter: null });
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false); // already running
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    const other = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution; // active
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false); // another parent is active: the partial index refuses, the claim loses
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(repo.claimResume(other.id)).toBe(false); // running is not resumable
    expect(repo.claimResume('missing')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('two repositories on two connections to one file: the second claim loses', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const a = freshDb(path);
    const b = openDatabase(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(a, clock);
    new ExecutionsRepo(a, clock).setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(b, clock).get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('interrupted');
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(a, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(b, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false);
    b.close();
    a.close();
  });

  it('two concurrent claimers in two threads: exactly one wins, every round', async () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = freshDb(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'skipped_window' });
    const contender = (id: string, barrier: SharedArrayBuffer) =>
      new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
        const w = new Worker(new URL('./helpers/claim-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), { workerData: { path, sql: CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, id, barrier } });
        w.once('message', resolve);
        w.once('error', reject);
      });
    for (let round = 0; round < 10; round++) {
      const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { definition });
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
      const barrier = new SharedArrayBuffer(4);
      const results = await Promise.all([contender(execution.id, barrier), contender(execution.id, barrier)]);
      expect(results.sort()).toEqual([0, 1]);
      expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('running');
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    }
    db.close();
  });

  it('bootSweep: running and pending-before-boot become interrupted with auto_resume_count + 1', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: running } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'running' });
    const oldPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell() }).execution;
    const paused = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'running', cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    repo.setStatus(paused.id, 'paused_budget');
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const boot = clock.now();
    const freshPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29') }).execution;
    expect(repo.bootSweep(boot).interrupted.sort()).toEqual([oldPending.id, running.id].sort());
    expect(repo.get(running.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(oldPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(freshPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'pending', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.get(paused.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_budget', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.incrementAutoResume(running.id)).toBe(2);
    expect(() => repo.incrementAutoResume('missing')).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/db/src/repositories/executions.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import {
  type CellKey, type Clock, type DefinitionSnapshot, type Estimate, type ExecutionStatus, type Iata, type IsoDate, type IsoDateTime,
  RESUMABLE_STATUSES, type Stage, stripUrlsInText,
} from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../schema/checks.js';
import type { Execution, ExecutionCreateInput, ExecutionPatch, ListExecutionsOptions } from '../types.js';

interface ExecutionDbRow {
  id: string; definition_id: string; is_child: number; child_origin: string | null; child_destination: string | null;
  child_departure_date: string | null; child_return_date: string | null; definition_snapshot_json: string; status: ExecutionStatus;
  stage: Stage | null; estimate_json: string | null; cancel_requested: number; resume_after: string | null; auto_resume_count: number;
  started_at: string; finished_at: string | null; error_text: string | null;
}

function toExecution(r: ExecutionDbRow): Execution {
  const cell: CellKey | null =
    r.is_child === 1 && r.child_origin !== null && r.child_destination !== null && r.child_departure_date !== null && r.child_return_date !== null
      ? { origin: r.child_origin as Iata, destination: r.child_destination as Iata, departureDate: r.child_departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.child_return_date as IsoDate }
      : null;
  return {
    id: r.id, definitionId: r.definition_id, isChild: r.is_child === 1, cell,
    // why: the snapshot was validated by zod and the engine before it was frozen; it is read back as written.
    snapshot: JSON.parse(r.definition_snapshot_json) as DefinitionSnapshot,
    status: r.status, stage: r.stage, estimate: r.estimate_json === null ? null : (JSON.parse(r.estimate_json) as Estimate),
    cancelRequested: r.cancel_requested === 1, resumeAfter: r.resume_after as IsoDateTime | null, autoResumeCount: r.auto_resume_count,
    startedAt: r.started_at as IsoDateTime, finishedAt: r.finished_at as IsoDateTime | null, errorText: r.error_text,
  };
}

const quoted = (xs: readonly string[]) => xs.map((x) => `'${x}'`).join(', ');

/**
 * Spec §8.1 "Resume is a compare-and-set": the tick, the one-shot job, boot auto-resume and
 * `/resume` all run this one statement; exactly one caller sees `changes = 1`. Clearing
 * `resume_after` in the same statement cancels the one-shot schedule in the same step.
 */
export const CLAIM_RESUME_SQL = `UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'running', resume_after = NULL WHERE id = ? AND status IN (${quoted(RESUMABLE_STATUSES)})`;

const SKIPPED_AT_CREATE: ReadonlySet<ExecutionCreateInput['status']> = new Set(['skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed']);

/** Spec §7 `search_executions`. Every write is a single statement or one transaction. */
export class ExecutionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  create(input: ExecutionCreateInput): Execution {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const c = input.cell;
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, is_child, child_origin, child_destination, child_departure_date, child_return_date,
          definition_snapshot_json, status, estimate_json, started_at, finished_at, error_text)
         VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @isChild, @o, @d, @dep, @ret, @snapshot, @status, @estimate, @now, @finishedAt, @errorText)`,
      )
      .run({
        id, definitionId: input.definitionId, isChild: c === null ? 0 : 1,
        o: c?.origin ?? null, d: c?.destination ?? null, dep: c?.departureDate ?? null, ret: c?.returnDate ?? null,
        snapshot: JSON.stringify(input.snapshot), status: input.status, estimate: input.estimate === null ? null : JSON.stringify(input.estimate),
        now, finishedAt: SKIPPED_AT_CREATE.has(input.status) ? now : null,
        errorText: input.errorText === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(input.errorText),
      });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  get(id: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  listForDefinition(id: string, opts: ListExecutionsOptions): Execution[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @id AND (@includeChildren = 1 OR is_child = 0)
         ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT @limit`,
      )
      .all({ id, includeChildren: opts.includeChildren ? 1 : 0, limit: opts.limit })
      .map(toExecution);
  }

  activeParent(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`)
      .get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  activeChild(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @definitionId AND is_child = 1 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})
         AND child_origin = @origin AND child_destination = @destination AND child_departure_date = @departureDate AND child_return_date = @returnDate`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  latestCompleted(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status = 'completed' ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`)
      .get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 compare-and-set. A claim that would create a second active parent (or child) loses instead of throwing. */
  claimResume(id: string): boolean {
    try {
      return this.db.raw.prepare(CLAIM_RESUME_SQL).run(id).changes === 1;
    } catch (err) {
      // why: `/resume` on a completed run while another run is active must lose like any other claimer (409), not crash.
      if (err instanceof Database.SqliteError && err.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE') return false;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Sets status and exactly the patch keys present. `error_text` is URL-stripped (spec §5.3 secret scrubbing). */
  setStatus(id: string, status: ExecutionStatus, patch: ExecutionPatch = {}): void {
    const sets = ['status = @status'];
    const params: Record<string, unknown> = { id, status };
    if ('stage' in patch) {
      sets.push('stage = @stage');
      params.stage = patch.stage ?? null;
    }
    if ('resumeAfter' in patch) {
      assertNullableIsoDateTime('resume_after', patch.resumeAfter ?? null);
      sets.push('resume_after = @resumeAfter');
      params.resumeAfter = patch.resumeAfter ?? null;
    }
    if ('finishedAt' in patch) {
      assertNullableIsoDateTime('finished_at', patch.finishedAt ?? null);
      sets.push('finished_at = @finishedAt');
      params.finishedAt = patch.finishedAt ?? null;
    }
    if ('errorText' in patch) {
      sets.push('error_text = @errorText');
      params.errorText = patch.errorText === null || patch.errorText === undefined ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.errorText);
    }
    if ('cancelRequested' in patch) {
      sets.push('cancel_requested = @cancelRequested');
      params.cancelRequested = patch.cancelRequested ? 1 : 0;
    }
    if ('estimate' in patch) {
      sets.push('estimate_json = @estimate');
      params.estimate = patch.estimate === null || patch.estimate === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(patch.estimate);
    }
    const changes = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_executions SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE id = @id`).run(params).changes;
    if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /**
   * Spec §8.1 "Boot order": `running` executions and `pending` ones created before this boot become
   * `interrupted`, and these crash-marked rows increment `auto_resume_count`; one atomic statement.
   */
  bootSweep(bootAt: IsoDateTime): { interrupted: string[] } {
    assertIsoDateTime('bootAt', bootAt);
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { id: string }>(
        `UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'interrupted', auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1
         WHERE status = 'running' OR (status = 'pending' AND started_at < ?) RETURNING id`,
      )
      .all(bootAt);
    return { interrupted: rows.map((r) => r.id) };
  }

  incrementAutoResume(id: string): number {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { n: number }>('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1 WHERE id = ? RETURNING auto_resume_count AS n')
      .get(id);
    if (r === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return r.n;
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): Execution {
    const e = this.get(id);
    if (e === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return e;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { type CellOutcomeStatus, type ProviderId, type RollUp, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cellKeyOf } from '../src/internal/cellKey.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

type Variant = { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; minPence: number | null };
const VARIANTS: Variant[] = [
  { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 500 },
  { status: 'empty', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'no_data', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', minPence: null },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'auth', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'budget', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'departed', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'not_selected', minPence: null },
];

/** Spec §6.6 precedence table, restated independently of the SQL (rows 0–6). */
function reference(outs: Variant[]): RollUp {
  if (outs.length === 0) return { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null }; // row 0
  const has = (s: CellOutcomeStatus) => outs.some((o) => o.status === s);
  if (has('priced')) return { status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: Math.min(...outs.filter((o) => o.status === 'priced').map((o) => o.minPence ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)) };
  if (has('not_run')) return { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null };
  if (has('error')) return { status: 'error', reason: outs.filter((o) => o.status === 'error').map((o) => o.reason ?? '').sort()[0] ?? null, minPence: null };
  if (has('empty')) return { status: 'empty', reason: null, minPence: null };
  if (has('no_data')) return { status: 'no_data', reason: null, minPence: null };
  const priority = ['budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed', 'not_selected'];
  return { status: 'skipped', reason: priority.find((r) => outs.some((o) => o.reason === r)) ?? null, minPence: null };
}

const PROVIDERS: ProviderId[] = ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi'];

describe('CellsRepo seeding and roll-ups (spec §3, §6.6, §7)', () => {
  it('seed writes cells and not_run outcomes per discovery provider; verification has no rows (row 0 → NULL)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock);
    repo.seed(execution.id, [cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)], PROVIDERS);
    expect(repo.outcomes(execution.id)).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(repo.outcomes(execution.id, { providerId: 'searchapi', cell: cell() })).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ ...cell(), executionId: execution.id, stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'searchapi', status: 'not_run', attempts: 0, reason: null }),
    ]);
    const r = repo.rollUps(execution.id).get(cellKeyOf(cell()));
    expect(r).toEqual({ discovery: { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null }, verification: { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null } });
    expect(repo.buckets(execution.id, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 0 });
  });

  it('a stage with no provider configured has no outcome rows and a NULL roll-up', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock);
    repo.seed(execution.id, [cell()], []);
    expect(repo.outcomes(execution.id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(execution.id).get(cellKeyOf(cell()))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: null, reason: null, minPence: null });
    expect(repo.buckets(execution.id, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 0, reused: 0 });
  });

  it('precedence over every pair of outcomes (100 combinations) matches the independently stated table', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock);
    for (const [i, a] of VARIANTS.entries()) {
      const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' });
      repo.seed(execution.id, VARIANTS.map((_, j) => cell(undefined, undefined, `2027-03-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`, `2027-04-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`)), PROVIDERS);
      for (const [j, b] of VARIANTS.entries()) {
        const c = cell(undefined, undefined, `2027-03-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`, `2027-04-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`);
        const bOut = { ...b, minPence: b.status === 'priced' ? 400 : null };
        repo.setOutcome(execution.id, c, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: a.status, reason: a.reason, minPence: a.minPence });
        repo.setOutcome(execution.id, c, 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: bOut.status, reason: bOut.reason, minPence: bOut.minPence });
        expect({ i, j, roll: repo.rollUps(execution.id).get(cellKeyOf(c))?.discovery }).toEqual({ i, j, roll: reference([a, bOut]) });
      }
    }
  });

  it('setOutcome increments attempts on request, strips URLs from last_error, and rejects unknown rows and skip reasons', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock);
    repo.seed(execution.id, [cell()], ['searchapi']);
    repo.setOutcome(execution.id, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'error', reason: 'network', lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search?api_key=SECRET', incrementAttempts: true });
    const [o] = repo.outcomes(execution.id);
    expect(o).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', reason: 'network', attempts: 1, lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search' });
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(execution.id, cell(undefined, EWR), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'priced' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(execution.id, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'skipped', reason: 'bored' })).toThrow(/skip reason/);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(execution.id, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { minPence: 1.5 })).toThrow(/min_pence/);
  });
});
```
