`packages/db/src/schema/searchCells.ts`:

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { OUTCOME_STATUSES, isBool, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_cells`: one row per cell of an execution with the stored roll-ups (spec §6.6).
 * `*_reason` and `verification_min_pence` hold the rest of the `RollUp` (status, reason, minPence);
 * `NULL` status = the stage has no provider or no outcome rows yet. `best_observation_id` gains its
 * foreign key in Task 3.14, when `price_observations` exists.
 */
export const searchCells = sqliteTable(
  'search_cells',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    discoveryStatus: text('discovery_status'),
    discoveryReason: text('discovery_reason'),
    discoveryMinPence: integer('discovery_min_pence'),
    verificationSelected: integer('verification_selected').notNull().default(0),
    selectionReason: text('selection_reason'),
    verificationStatus: text('verification_status'),
    verificationReason: text('verification_reason'),
    verificationMinPence: integer('verification_min_pence'),
    bestObservationId: integer('best_observation_id'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate] }),
    check('search_cells_discovery_status_ck', isOneOf('discovery_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_verification_status_ck', isOneOf('verification_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_selected_ck', isBool('verification_selected')),
    check('search_cells_selection_reason_ck', isOneOf('selection_reason', ['always_include', 'top_n', 'ceiling', 'stride'], true)),
    check('search_cells_discovery_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('discovery_min_pence')),
    check('search_cells_verification_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('verification_min_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/cellOutcomes.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, foreignKey, index, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, OUTCOME_STATUSES, POINTS_OF_SALE, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchCells } from './searchCells.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `cell_outcomes`: per (execution, cell, stage, provider). `attempts` counts engine passes. */
export const cellOutcomes = sqliteTable(
  'cell_outcomes',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    stage: text('stage').notNull(),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    reason: text('reason'),
    attempts: integer('attempts').notNull().default(0),
    lastError: text('last_error'),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at'),
    minPence: integer('min_pence'),
    cabin: text('cabin'),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale'),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.stage, t.providerId] }),
    foreignKey({
      columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate],
      foreignColumns: [searchCells.executionId, searchCells.origin, searchCells.destination, searchCells.departureDate, searchCells.returnDate],
    }),
    check('cell_outcomes_status_ck', isOneOf('status', OUTCOME_STATUSES)),
    check('cell_outcomes_stage_ck', isOneOf('stage', ['discovery', 'verification'])),
    check('cell_outcomes_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('cell_outcomes_skip_reason_ck', sql.raw("status <> 'skipped' OR reason IN ('not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed')")),
    check('cell_outcomes_attempts_ck', isInteger('attempts')),
    check('cell_outcomes_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('min_pence')),
    check('cell_outcomes_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_pos_ck', isOneOf('point_of_sale', POINTS_OF_SALE, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_kind_ck', isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_fetched_ck', isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at', true)),
    index('cell_outcomes_stage_provider_status_idx').on(t.executionId, t.stage, t.providerId, t.status),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/internal/cellKey.ts`:

```ts
import type { CellKey } from '@gmt/core';

/** `O|D|dep|ret`, the same string as WP1's `cellKeyString` (plan WP1 Produces), used as Map keys. */
export function cellKeyOf(c: CellKey): string {
  return `${c.origin}|${c.destination}|${c.departureDate}|${c.returnDate}`;
}
```

`packages/db/src/internal/rollUpSql.ts`:

```ts
import type { Stage } from '@gmt/core';

/**
 * Spec §6.6 roll-up precedence as one correlated UPDATE over `search_cells`, run inside every write
 * transaction that changes outcomes (spec §3 "stored, recomputed with every write"):
 *   0 no outcome rows → NULL; 1 any priced; 2 any not_run; 3 any error; 4 any empty (others ⊆ {empty,
 *   no_data, skipped}); 5 any no_data; 6 all skipped, reason by budget > provider_disabled > departed > not_selected.
 * `min_pence` is the minimum over priced outcomes; an error roll-up carries the smallest error reason.
 */
export function rollUpUpdateSql(stage: Stage, scope: 'execution' | 'cell'): string {
  const from = `FROM cell_outcomes o WHERE o.execution_id = search_cells.execution_id AND o.origin = search_cells.origin
    AND o.destination = search_cells.destination AND o.departure_date = search_cells.departure_date
    AND o.return_date = search_cells.return_date AND o.stage = '${stage}'`;
  return `UPDATE search_cells SET
  ${stage}_status = (SELECT CASE
      WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'priced') > 0 THEN 'priced'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'not_run') > 0 THEN 'not_run'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'error') > 0 THEN 'error'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'empty') > 0 THEN 'empty'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'no_data') > 0 THEN 'no_data'
      ELSE 'skipped' END ${from}),
  ${stage}_reason = (SELECT CASE
      WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status IN ('priced', 'not_run')) > 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'error') > 0 THEN MIN(CASE WHEN o.status = 'error' THEN o.reason END)
      WHEN SUM(o.status IN ('empty', 'no_data')) > 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'budget') > 0 THEN 'budget'
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'provider_disabled') > 0 THEN 'provider_disabled'
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'departed') > 0 THEN 'departed'
      ELSE 'not_selected' END ${from}),
  ${stage}_min_pence = (SELECT MIN(CASE WHEN o.status = 'priced' THEN o.min_pence END) ${from})
WHERE execution_id = @executionId${scope === 'cell' ? ' AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate' : ''}`;
}
```

`packages/db/src/internal/outcomeRow.ts` (shared by `CellsRepo`, `ItinerariesRepo` and `CalendarPricesRepo`, so no repository imports another for a row type):

```ts
import type { Cabin, CellOutcome, CellOutcomeStatus, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, PointOfSale, ProviderId, SourceKind, Stage } from '@gmt/core';

/** A `cell_outcomes` row as SQLite returns it. */
export interface OutcomeDbRow {
  execution_id: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; stage: Stage; provider_id: ProviderId;
  status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; attempts: number; last_error: string | null; fetched_at: string | null; min_pence: number | null;
  cabin: Cabin | null; point_of_sale: PointOfSale | null; source_kind: SourceKind | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

export function toOutcome(r: OutcomeDbRow): CellOutcome {
  return {
    executionId: r.execution_id, origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate,
    stage: r.stage, providerId: r.provider_id, status: r.status, reason: r.reason, attempts: r.attempts, lastError: r.last_error,
    fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime | null, minPence: r.min_pence, cabin: r.cabin, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, sourceKind: r.source_kind,
    reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (Task 3.10 methods; Tasks 3.12–3.14 add `writeTile`, `seedVerification`, `selected`, `resumable`, `markDeparted`, `writeVerifiedCell` to this class):

```ts
import {
  type Buckets, type Cabin, type CellKey, type CellOutcome, type CellOutcomeStatus, type Clock, type Iata, type IsoDate,
  type PointOfSale, type ProviderId, type RollUp, type SourceKind, type Stage, stripUrlsInText,
} from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { cellKeyOf } from '../internal/cellKey.js';
import { type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';
import { rollUpUpdateSql } from '../internal/rollUpSql.js';
import { RowRejected, assertNullableInt, assertNullableIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { OutcomeFilter, OutcomePatch } from '../types.js';

interface CellDbRow {
  origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string;
  discovery_status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; discovery_reason: string | null; discovery_min_pence: number | null;
  verification_status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; verification_reason: string | null; verification_min_pence: number | null;
}

const SKIP_REASONS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed']);

const CELL_WHERE = 'execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate';

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_cells` + `cell_outcomes` and the stored roll-ups. Every method that changes an
 * outcome recomputes the affected roll-ups in the same transaction.
 */
export class CellsRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: { syntheticAllowed: boolean } = { syntheticAllowed: false },
  ) {}

  /** Spec §7 atomic unit "seeding search_cells + discovery cell_outcomes at start". */
  seed(executionId: string, cells: readonly CellKey[], discoveryProviders: readonly ProviderId[]): void {
    const insertCell = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_cells (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, discovery_status, verification_selected)
       VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @status, 0)`,
    );
    const insertOutcome = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, attempts)
       VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, 'discovery', @providerId, 'not_run', 0)`,
    );
    const status = discoveryProviders.length > 0 ? 'not_run' : null;
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of cells) {
        const key = { executionId, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate };
        insertCell.run({ ...key, status });
        for (const providerId of discoveryProviders) insertOutcome.run({ ...key, providerId });
      }
    });
  }

  outcomes(executionId: string, filter: OutcomeFilter = {}): CellOutcome[] {
    const c = filter.cell;
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId
           AND (@stage IS NULL OR stage = @stage) AND (@providerId IS NULL OR provider_id = @providerId) AND (@status IS NULL OR status = @status)
           AND (@origin IS NULL OR (origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate))
         ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id`,
      )
      .all({
        executionId, stage: filter.stage ?? null, providerId: filter.providerId ?? null, status: filter.status ?? null,
        origin: c?.origin ?? null, destination: c?.destination ?? null, departureDate: c?.departureDate ?? null, returnDate: c?.returnDate ?? null,
      })
      .map(toOutcome);
  }

  /** Changes one outcome and its cell's roll-up in one transaction. Only the patch keys present are written. */
  setOutcome(executionId: string, cell: CellKey, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, patch: OutcomePatch): void {
    if (patch.status === 'skipped' && (patch.reason === undefined || patch.reason === null || !SKIP_REASONS.has(patch.reason))) {
      throw new RowRejected('skip-reason', `a skipped outcome needs a skip reason (spec §3), got ${String(patch.reason)}`);
    }
    if (patch.minPence !== undefined) assertNullableInt('min_pence', patch.minPence);
    if (patch.fetchedAt !== undefined) assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', patch.fetchedAt);
    const sets: string[] = [];
    const params: Record<string, unknown> = { executionId, stage, providerId, ...cell };
    const put = (column: string, key: string, value: unknown) => {
      sets.push(`${column} = @${key}`);
      params[key] = value;
    };
    if (patch.status !== undefined) put('status', 'status', patch.status);
    if (patch.reason !== undefined) put('reason', 'reason', patch.reason);
    if (patch.lastError !== undefined) put('last_error', 'lastError', patch.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.lastError));
    if (patch.fetchedAt !== undefined) put('fetched_at', 'fetchedAt', patch.fetchedAt);
    if (patch.minPence !== undefined) put('min_pence', 'minPence', patch.minPence);
    if (patch.cabin !== undefined) put('cabin', 'cabin', patch.cabin);
    if (patch.pointOfSale !== undefined) put('point_of_sale', 'pointOfSale', patch.pointOfSale);
    if (patch.sourceKind !== undefined) put('source_kind', 'sourceKind', patch.sourceKind);
    if (patch.reusedFromExecutionId !== undefined) put('reused_from_execution_id', 'reusedFrom', patch.reusedFromExecutionId);
    if (patch.incrementAttempts === true) sets.push('attempts = attempts + 1');
    if (sets.length === 0) return;
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`).run(params).changes;
      if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no ${stage} outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyOf(cell)} in ${executionId}`);
      this.recompute(executionId, stage, cell);
    });
  }

  rollUps(executionId: string): Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }> {
    const rows = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], CellDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date').all(executionId);
    const out = new Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }>();
    for (const r of rows) {
      out.set(`${r.origin}|${r.destination}|${r.departure_date}|${r.return_date}`, {
        discovery: { status: r.discovery_status, reason: r.discovery_reason, minPence: r.discovery_min_pence },
        verification: { status: r.verification_status, reason: r.verification_reason, minPence: r.verification_min_pence },
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** The six buckets of spec §3 plus `reused` for one stage and provider. */
  buckets(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId): Buckets {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, Record<keyof Buckets, number | null>>(
        `SELECT SUM(status = 'priced') AS priced, SUM(status = 'empty') AS empty, SUM(status = 'no_data') AS no_data, SUM(status = 'error') AS error,
                SUM(status = 'skipped') AS skipped, SUM(status = 'not_run') AS not_run, SUM(reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL) AS reused
         FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`,
      )
      .get({ executionId, stage, providerId });
    return {
      priced: r?.priced ?? 0, empty: r?.empty ?? 0, no_data: r?.no_data ?? 0, error: r?.error ?? 0,
      skipped: r?.skipped ?? 0, not_run: r?.not_run ?? 0, reused: r?.reused ?? 0,
    };
  }

  /** Recomputes stored roll-ups for one cell or the whole execution. Callers are inside a transaction. */
  protected recompute(executionId: string, stage: Stage, cell?: CellKey): void {
    if (cell === undefined) this.db.raw.prepare(rollUpUpdateSql(stage, 'execution')).run({ executionId });
    else this.db.raw.prepare(rollUpUpdateSql(stage, 'cell')).run({ executionId, origin: cell.origin, destination: cell.destination, departureDate: cell.departureDate, returnDate: cell.returnDate });
  }
}
```

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, type Provenance, asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Db } from '../src/client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { calendarPrice, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const prov = (p: Partial<Provenance>): Provenance => ({ ...calendarPrice(cell()).provenance, ...p });
const sqliteCode = (fn: () => unknown): string | null => {
  try {
    fn();
    return null;
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof Database.SqliteError ? e.code : `not a SqliteError: ${String(e)}`;
  }
};

function setup(syntheticAllowed = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, executionId: execution.id, repo: new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, { syntheticAllowed }) };
}

/** Raw insert bypassing the repository, to prove the SQL constraints hold on their own. */
function rawInsert(db: Db, executionId: string, over: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const row = {
    execution_id: executionId, provider_id: 'travelpayouts', source_kind: 'authorised-cached', cabin: 'economy', origin: 'LHR', destination: 'JFK',
    departure_date: '2027-03-14', return_date: '2027-03-28', price_pence: 41_200, price_native_minor: 41_200, currency_native: 'GBP',
    fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: null, fx_source: null, fx_converted: 0, point_of_sale: 'unknown', fetched_at: T0, ...over,
  };
  const cols = Object.keys(row);
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO calendar_prices (${cols.join(', ')}) VALUES (${cols.map((c) => `@${c}`).join(', ')})`).run(row);
}

describe('calendar_prices writes (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7)', () => {
  it('stores provenance explicitly: max_age_hours NULL for unknown, FX columns when converted', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    repo.insert([
      { ...calendarPrice(cell()), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null },
      {
        ...calendarPrice(cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'), {
          provenance: prov({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', maxAgeHours: 'unknown', pointOfSale: 'GB', fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 750_000_000, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'frankfurter' } }),
          currencyNative: 'USD', priceNativeMinor: 54_900, pricePence: 41_175,
        }),
        executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null,
      },
    ]);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT provider_id, max_age_hours, fx_converted, fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, currency_native, price_pence FROM calendar_prices ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([
      { provider_id: 'travelpayouts', max_age_hours: 48, fx_converted: 0, fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: null, fx_source: null, currency_native: 'GBP', price_pence: 41_200 },
      { provider_id: 'searchapi', max_age_hours: null, fx_converted: 1, fx_rate_nano: 750_000_000, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'frankfurter', currency_native: 'USD', price_pence: 41_175 },
    ]);
  });

  it('provider ⇔ kind, both directions, in the repository and in SQL', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup(true);
    const write = (p: Partial<Provenance>) => () => repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { provenance: prov(p) }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(write({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'fixture', source_kind: 'authorised-cached' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'searchapi', source_kind: 'synthetic' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).not.toThrow(); // allowed under the gate
  });

  it('synthetic rows are refused when syntheticAllowed is false (the default)', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup(false);
    expect(() => repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { provenance: prov({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' }) }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }])).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }),
    );
  });

  it('FX CHECK constraints, both directions, and price_pence = price_native_minor when unconverted', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    // SQL: converted ⇔ currency ≠ GBP
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: 1, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 0, currency_native: 'USD' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    // SQL: converted rows carry rate, date and source
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'USD', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    // repository: the same rules plus pence = native when unconverted
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), over), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    expect(write({ currencyNative: 'USD' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'fx' }));
    expect(write({ pricePence: 41_200, priceNativeMinor: 41_201 })).toThrow(/must equal price_native_minor/);
    expect(write({ provenance: prov({ fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 1, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'x' } }) })).toThrow(/GBP price cannot be FX-converted/);
  });

  it('price tables reject non-integers and non-toISOString timestamps (repository and SQL)', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), over), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    expect(write({ pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: prov({ fetchedAt: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' as IsoDateTime }) })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'timestamp' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: prov({ maxAgeHours: 1.5 }) })).toThrow(/max_age_hours/);
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { price_pence: 412.5, price_native_minor: 412.5 }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14 12:00:00.000' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices').get()).toEqual({ n: 0 });
  });

  it('UNIQUE (execution, provider, cell): a second row for the same key throws; another provider or execution does not', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell()), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    expect(sqliteCode(() => repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { provenance: prov({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', pointOfSale: 'GB' }) }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    const other = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' }).execution;
    repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell()), executionId: other.id, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices').get()).toEqual({ n: 3 });
  });
});

describe('plan Review Focus 5: a SqliteError inside a repository transaction', () => {
  it('propagates with its SQLite code and message, holds no URL, and rolls the whole transaction back', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const link = 'https://www.aviasales.com/search/LON1403NYC28031?marker=SECRET-TOKEN-0123456789';
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      db.transaction(() => {
        repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]);
        repo.insert([{ ...calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link }), executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }]); // same key
      });
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    const err = thrown as InstanceType<typeof Database.SqliteError>;
    expect(err.code).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    expect(err.message).toMatch(/^UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices\./);
    expect(err.message).not.toMatch(/https?:|SECRET|marker=/);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices').get()).toEqual({ n: 0 });
    // the engine records it on the execution (WP7); the column never holds a URL query or userinfo
    const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    executions.setStatus(executionId, 'paused_error', { errorText: `${err.message} while writing tile 3 (${link.replace('https://', 'https://user:pw@')})` });
    const text = executions.get(executionId)?.errorText ?? '';
    expect(text).toContain('UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices.');
    expect(text).not.toMatch(/SECRET|marker=|pw@/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/**
 * The CHECKs both price tables carry (spec §7): integer money, the two FX constraints verbatim,
 * timestamp form, explicit max-age. Constraint names are prefixed with the table name.
 */
export function priceTableChecks(table: string) {
  return [
    check(`${table}_price_ck`, isInteger('price_pence')),
    check(`${table}_native_ck`, isInteger('price_native_minor')),
    check(`${table}_fx_converted_ck`, sql.raw("(fx_converted = 1) = (currency_native <> 'GBP')")),
    check(`${table}_fx_complete_ck`, sql.raw('fx_converted = 0 OR (fx_rate_nano IS NOT NULL AND fx_rate_date IS NOT NULL AND fx_source IS NOT NULL)')),
    check(`${table}_fx_rate_ck`, isNullOrInteger('fx_rate_nano')),
    check(`${table}_fx_bool_ck`, isBool('fx_converted')),
    check(`${table}_currency_ck`, sql.raw("currency_native GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check(`${table}_fetched_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at')),
    check(`${table}_observed_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('provider_observed_at', true)),
    check(`${table}_max_age_ck`, isNullOrInteger('max_age_hours')),
    check(`${table}_cabin_ck`, isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check(`${table}_pos_ck`, isOneOf('point_of_sale', POINTS_OF_SALE)),
    check(`${table}_kind_ck`, isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS)),
  ];
}
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { PROVIDER_IDS, isOneOf, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `calendar_prices`: insert-only, one row per (execution, provider, cell). History and route
 * series read only `reused_from_execution_id IS NULL`.
 */
export const calendarPrices = sqliteTable(
  'calendar_prices',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    vendorLink: text('vendor_link'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('calendar_prices_execution_provider_cell_uq').on(t.executionId, t.providerId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate),
    index('calendar_prices_cell_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_route_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_execution_idx').on(t.executionId),
    ...priceTableChecks('calendar_prices'),
    check('calendar_prices_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    // why: spec §5.5 "provider_id = 'fixture' ⇔ source_kind = 'synthetic'", held in SQL as well as in the repository.
    check('calendar_prices_provider_kind_ck', sql.raw("(provider_id = 'fixture') = (source_kind = 'synthetic')")),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import type { Cabin, Clock, FxInfo, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, SourceKind } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { CalendarPriceRow, CalendarPriceWrite } from '../types.js';

export interface CalendarPriceDbRow {
  id: number; execution_id: string; provider_id: ProviderId; source_kind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; origin: string; destination: string;
  departure_date: string; return_date: string; price_pence: number; price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string;
  fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null; fx_converted: number; point_of_sale: PointOfSale;
  fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null; max_age_hours: number | null; vendor_link: string | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;

/** Every repository rule for a calendar price (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7). Throws `RowRejected`. */
export function validateCalendarPrice(row: CalendarPriceWrite, syntheticAllowed: boolean): void {
  const p = row.provenance;
  assertProviderKind(p.providerId, p.sourceKind, syntheticAllowed);
  assertPriceMoney({ pricePence: row.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: row.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: row.currencyNative, fx: p.fx });
  assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', p.fetchedAt);
  if (p.providerObservedAt !== undefined) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', p.providerObservedAt);
  if (p.maxAgeHours !== 'unknown' && (!Number.isSafeInteger(p.maxAgeHours) || p.maxAgeHours < 0)) {
    throw new RowRejected('integer', `max_age_hours must be an integer or 'unknown', got ${String(p.maxAgeHours)}`);
  }
  if (!IATA.test(row.origin) || !IATA.test(row.destination)) throw new RowRejected('iata', `bad cell airports ${row.origin}→${row.destination}`);
  assertIsoDate('departure_date', row.departureDate);
  assertIsoDate('return_date', row.returnDate);
}

const INSERT_SQL = `
INSERT INTO calendar_prices (execution_id, provider_id, source_kind, cabin, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, price_pence, price_native_minor,
  currency_native, fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, max_age_hours, vendor_link, reused_from_execution_id)
VALUES (@executionId, @providerId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor,
  @currencyNative, @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @maxAgeHours, @vendorLink, @reusedFrom)`;

/** Inserts one validated row; the caller owns the transaction. Returns the new id. */
export function insertCalendarPrice(db: Db, row: CalendarPriceWrite): number {
  const p = row.provenance;
  const fx = p.fx;
  return Number(
    db.raw.prepare(INSERT_SQL).run({
      executionId: row.executionId, providerId: p.providerId, sourceKind: p.sourceKind, cabin: p.cabin,
      origin: row.origin, destination: row.destination, departureDate: row.departureDate, returnDate: row.returnDate,
      pricePence: row.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: row.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: row.currencyNative,
      fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
      pointOfSale: p.pointOfSale, fetchedAt: p.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: p.providerObservedAt ?? null,
      // why: spec §7 "max_age_hours NULL means unknown and is written explicitly".
      maxAgeHours: p.maxAgeHours === 'unknown' ? null : p.maxAgeHours,
      vendorLink: row.vendorLink, reusedFrom: row.reusedFromExecutionId,
    }).lastInsertRowid,
  );
}

/** Rebuilds `Provenance` from the stored columns; nothing is defaulted (spec §3). */
export function toCalendarPriceRow(r: CalendarPriceDbRow): CalendarPriceRow {
  let fx: FxInfo = { converted: false };
  if (r.fx_converted === 1) {
    if (r.fx_rate_nano === null || r.fx_rate_date === null || r.fx_source === null) throw new Error(`calendar price ${r.id} is converted without rate data`);
    fx = { converted: true, rateNano: r.fx_rate_nano, rateDate: r.fx_rate_date as IsoDate, source: r.fx_source };
  }
  const provenance: Provenance = {
    providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime,
    ...(r.provider_observed_at === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt: r.provider_observed_at as IsoDateTime }),
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin: r.cabin,
  };
  return {
    id: r.id, executionId: r.execution_id, origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata,
    departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate, provenance,
    pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native,
    vendorLink: r.vendor_link, reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}

/** Spec §7 `calendar_prices`. Insert-only except the reused-row supersede (Task 3.15). */
export class CalendarPricesRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: { syntheticAllowed: boolean } = { syntheticAllowed: false },
  ) {}

  /** Validates every row first, then inserts all in one transaction. A UNIQUE conflict throws SqliteError. */
  insert(rows: readonly CalendarPriceWrite[]): void {
    for (const r of rows) validateCalendarPrice(r, this.opts.syntheticAllowed);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) insertCalendarPrice(this.db, r);
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `import { insertCalendarPrice, validateCalendarPrice } from './calendarPrices.js';` and `import type { CalendarPriceInsert, TileOutcome, TileWrite } from '../types.js';`):

```ts
const MERGE_RANK: Record<TileOutcome['status'], number> = { priced: 4, empty: 3, no_data: 2, error: 1 };

interface MergedOutcome {
  status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; lastError: string | null; fetchedAt: string | null; minPence: number | null;
  cabin: Cabin | null; pointOfSale: PointOfSale | null; sourceKind: SourceKind | null; attempts: number; reusedFrom: string | null;
}

/**
 * Spec §6.6 "Within one provider, monotone merge priced > empty > no_data > error, min_pence = min".
 * Tiles partition cells, so a second result for a cell is a safety net; `not_run` and `skipped` are
 * always replaced. Attempts count engine passes that ended in an error (spec §7).
 */
function mergeOutcome(existing: OutcomeDbRow, incoming: TileOutcome, pricePence: number | null): MergedOutcome {
  const attempts = existing.attempts + (incoming.countAttempt ? 1 : 0);
  const existingRank = (MERGE_RANK as Partial<Record<CellOutcomeStatus, number>>)[existing.status];
  if (existingRank !== undefined && existingRank > MERGE_RANK[incoming.status]) {
    return {
      status: existing.status, reason: existing.reason, lastError: existing.last_error, fetchedAt: existing.fetched_at, minPence: existing.min_pence,
      cabin: existing.cabin, pointOfSale: existing.point_of_sale, sourceKind: existing.source_kind, attempts, reusedFrom: existing.reused_from_execution_id,
    };
  }
  const minPence =
    incoming.status !== 'priced' || pricePence === null
      ? null
      : existing.status === 'priced' && existing.min_pence !== null
        ? Math.min(existing.min_pence, pricePence)
        : pricePence;
  return {
    status: incoming.status, reason: incoming.reason, lastError: incoming.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(incoming.lastError),
    fetchedAt: incoming.fetchedAt, minPence, cabin: incoming.cabin, pointOfSale: incoming.pointOfSale, sourceKind: incoming.sourceKind, attempts, reusedFrom: null,
  };
}

const UPDATE_MERGED_SQL = `
UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, last_error = @lastError, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence,
  cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, attempts = @attempts, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFrom
WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`;

const DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL = `DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND provider_id = @providerId AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`;
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one tile's prices, outcomes and roll-ups. Validation runs first (a malformed
   * tile writes nothing); any SQL failure inside rolls the whole tile back. A fresh price deletes a
   * reused row for the same cell in the same transaction. Committing a tile resets
   * `auto_resume_count` (spec §7 "reset when a tile or cell commits").
   */
  writeTile(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, result: TileWrite): void {
    const prices = new Map<string, CalendarPriceInsert>();
    for (const p of result.prices) {
      if (p.provenance.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('provider', `price from ${p.provenance.providerId} in a ${providerId} tile`);
      const key = cellKeyOf(p);
      if (prices.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('in-tile-conflict', `two prices for ${key} in one tile; the engine keeps one per cell (spec §5.6)`);
      validateCalendarPrice({ ...p, executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null }, this.opts.syntheticAllowed);
      prices.set(key, p);
    }
    const outcomeKeys = new Set<string>();
    for (const o of result.outcomes) {
      const key = cellKeyOf(o);
      if (outcomeKeys.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('in-tile-conflict', `two outcomes for ${key} in one tile`);
      outcomeKeys.add(key);
      assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', o.fetchedAt);
      if (o.status === 'priced' && !prices.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-price', `${key} is priced but the tile wrote no price for it (spec §3)`);
      if (o.status !== 'priced' && prices.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but its outcome is ${o.status}`);
    }
    for (const key of prices.keys()) {
      if (!outcomeKeys.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but no outcome`);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const p of result.prices) {
        this.db.raw.prepare(DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL).run({ executionId, providerId, origin: p.origin, destination: p.destination, departureDate: p.departureDate, returnDate: p.returnDate });
        insertCalendarPrice(this.db, { ...p, executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: null });
      }
      const select = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = 'discovery' AND provider_id = @providerId`);
      for (const o of result.outcomes) {
        const key = { executionId, providerId, origin: o.origin, destination: o.destination, departureDate: o.departureDate, returnDate: o.returnDate };
        const existing = select.get(key);
        if (existing === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded discovery outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyOf(o)}`);
        const merged = mergeOutcome(existing, o, prices.get(cellKeyOf(o))?.pricePence ?? null);
        this.db.raw.prepare(UPDATE_MERGED_SQL).run({ ...key, stage: 'discovery', ...merged });
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery', o);
      }
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }
```

```ts
import { type CellKey, type CellOutcomeStatus, asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cellKeyOf } from '../src/internal/cellKey.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const day = (n: number) => cell(undefined, undefined, `2027-05-${String(n).padStart(2, '0')}`, `2027-06-${String(n).padStart(2, '0')}`);

function setup(cells: CellKey[], discovery = ['searchapi'] as const) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock);
  repo.seed(execution.id, cells, [...discovery]);
  return { db, executionId: execution.id, repo };
}

describe('selection persistence (spec §6.7, §7 atomic unit "selection persistence + verification seeding")', () => {
  it('selected cells get not_run, the rest skipped:not_selected, roll-ups follow, and selection runs once', () => {
    const cells = [day(1), day(2), day(3)];
    const { executionId, repo } = setup(cells);
    repo.seedVerification(executionId, [{ ...day(1), reason: 'always_include' }, { ...day(3), reason: 'top_n' }], ['searchapi']);
    expect(repo.selected(executionId)).toEqual([{ ...day(1), reason: 'always_include' }, { ...day(3), reason: 'top_n' }]);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification' }).map((o) => [o.departureDate, o.status, o.reason])).toEqual([
      ['2027-05-01', 'not_run', null], ['2027-05-02', 'skipped', 'not_selected'], ['2027-05-03', 'not_run', null],
    ]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyOf(day(2)))?.verification).toEqual({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'not_selected', minPence: null });
    expect(() => repo.seedVerification(executionId, [], ['searchapi'])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'selection-once' }));
  });

  it('a selection naming a cell outside the execution writes nothing', () => {
    const { executionId, repo } = setup([day(1), day(2)]);
    expect(() => repo.seedVerification(executionId, [{ ...day(1), reason: 'top_n' }, { ...day(9), reason: 'top_n' }], ['searchapi'])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'selection-member' }));
    expect(repo.selected(executionId)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification' })).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('with no verification provider the selection is stored and the verification roll-up stays NULL', () => {
    const { executionId, repo } = setup([day(1)]);
    repo.seedVerification(executionId, [{ ...day(1), reason: 'stride' }], []);
    expect(repo.selected(executionId)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyOf(day(1)))?.verification.status).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('resume query per (stage, provider) (spec §7 "Resume")', () => {
  // n, status, reason, attempts, auto, manual
  const cases: Array<[number, CellOutcomeStatus, string | null, number, boolean, boolean]> = [
    [1, 'not_run', null, 0, true, true],
    [2, 'error', 'network', 2, true, true],
    [3, 'error', 'network', 3, false, false], // attempts exhausted
    [4, 'error', null, 0, true, true],
    [5, 'error', 'auth', 1, false, true],
    [6, 'error', 'quota', 1, false, true],
    [7, 'error', 'parse', 1, false, true],
    [8, 'error', 'no-return-legs', 1, false, false],
    [9, 'error', 'fx-unavailable', 1, false, false],
    [10, 'error', 'currency-unsupported', 1, false, false],
    [11, 'error', 'unknown-airport-tz', 1, false, false],
    [12, 'skipped', 'budget', 0, true, true],
    [13, 'skipped', 'provider_disabled', 0, false, true],
    [14, 'skipped', 'not_selected', 0, false, false],
    [15, 'skipped', 'departed', 0, false, false],
    [16, 'priced', null, 0, false, false],
    [17, 'empty', null, 0, false, false],
    [18, 'no_data', null, 0, false, false],
  ];

  it('automatic and manual resume select exactly the spec rows; priced, empty and no_data are never selected', () => {
    const { executionId, repo } = setup(cases.map(([n]) => day(n)));
    for (const [n, status, reason, attempts] of cases) {
      repo.setOutcome(executionId, day(n), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status, reason, minPence: status === 'priced' ? 100 : null });
      for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i++) repo.setOutcome(executionId, day(n), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { incrementAttempts: true });
    }
    const dates = (cells: CellKey[]) => cells.map((c) => Number(c.departureDate.slice(8)));
    expect(dates(repo.resumable(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', false))).toEqual(cases.filter((c) => c[4]).map((c) => c[0]));
    expect(dates(repo.resumable(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', true))).toEqual(cases.filter((c) => c[5]).map((c) => c[0]));
    expect(repo.resumable(executionId, 'verification', 'searchapi', true)).toEqual([]); // per stage
    expect(repo.resumable(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', true)).toEqual([]); // per provider
  });
});

describe('markDeparted (spec §8.1 "Before planning … become skipped:departed")', () => {
  it('turns not_run and skipped outcomes departing on or before today into skipped:departed, in both stages', () => {
    const d14 = cell(undefined, undefined, '2026-10-14', '2026-10-28');
    const d15 = cell(undefined, undefined, '2026-10-15', '2026-10-29');
    const d16 = cell(undefined, undefined, '2026-10-16', '2026-10-30');
    const d14b = cell(undefined, EWR, '2026-10-14', '2026-10-28');
    const { executionId, repo } = setup([d14, d15, d16, d14b]);
    repo.seedVerification(executionId, [{ ...d15, reason: 'top_n' }], ['searchapi']);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, d14b, 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'priced', minPence: 30_000 });
    // changed: d14 discovery not_run, d14 verification skipped:not_selected, d15 discovery not_run, d15 verification not_run,
    // d14b verification skipped:not_selected; d14b discovery is priced and stays
    expect(repo.markDeparted(executionId, asIsoDate('2026-10-15'))).toBe(5);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { cell: d14 }).map((o) => [o.stage, o.status, o.reason])).toEqual([['discovery', 'skipped', 'departed'], ['verification', 'skipped', 'departed']]);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { cell: d14b, stage: 'discovery' })[0]?.status).toBe('priced'); // untouched
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { cell: d16, stage: 'discovery' })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyOf(d14))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'departed', minPence: null });
    expect(repo.markDeparted(executionId, asIsoDate('2026-10-15'))).toBe(0); // idempotent
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (import `SelectionReason` from `@gmt/core`):

```ts
/** Spec §7 "Resume": error reasons never retried automatically. Manual resume re-admits auth, quota, parse. */
const NON_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse', 'no-return-legs', 'fx-unavailable', 'currency-unsupported', 'unknown-airport-tz'] as const;
const MANUAL_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse'] as const;

/**
 * Spec §7 resume selection for one (stage, provider). `skipped:budget` is returned here; the engine
 * keeps it only when headroom exists (spec §7 "when headroom exists" is a budget question, WP7).
 * `priced | empty | no_data` are never returned.
 */
export const RESUMABLE_SQL = `
SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date FROM cell_outcomes
WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND (
  status = 'not_run'
  OR (status = 'error' AND attempts < 3 AND (reason IS NULL OR reason NOT IN (${NON_RETRYABLE.map((r) => `'${r}'`).join(', ')})))
  OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'budget')
  OR (@manual = 1 AND ((status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'provider_disabled') OR (status = 'error' AND reason IN (${MANUAL_RETRYABLE.map((r) => `'${r}'`).join(', ')}))))
)
ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date`;

interface CellKeyDbRow { origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string }
const toCellKey = (r: CellKeyDbRow): CellKey => ({ origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate });
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "selection persistence + verification seeding": marks the selection, seeds
   * one verification outcome per cell × provider (selected → not_run, others → skipped:not_selected)
   * and recomputes roll-ups. Selection runs once per execution (spec §6.7); a second call is refused.
   */
  seedVerification(executionId: string, selection: ReadonlyArray<CellKey & { reason: SelectionReason }>, verificationProviders: readonly ProviderId[]): void {
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      const existing = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ? AND stage = 'verification'`).get(executionId)?.n ?? 0;
      if (existing > 0) throw new RowRejected('selection-once', `verification outcomes already exist for ${executionId}; selection runs once per execution`);
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 0, selection_reason = NULL WHERE execution_id = ?').run(executionId);
      const mark = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 1, selection_reason = @reason WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`);
      for (const s of selection) {
        const changes = mark.run({ executionId, origin: s.origin, destination: s.destination, departureDate: s.departureDate, returnDate: s.returnDate, reason: s.reason }).changes;
        if (changes !== 1) throw new RowRejected('selection-member', `${cellKeyOf(s)} is not a cell of ${executionId}`);
      }
      const seed = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, reason, attempts)
         SELECT execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, 'verification', @providerId,
                CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN 'not_run' ELSE 'skipped' END,
                CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN NULL ELSE 'not_selected' END, 0
         FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = @executionId`,
      );
      for (const providerId of verificationProviders) seed.run({ executionId, providerId });
      this.recompute(executionId, 'verification');
    });
  }

  selected(executionId: string): Array<CellKey & { reason: SelectionReason }> {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], CellKeyDbRow & { selection_reason: SelectionReason }>(
        'SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, selection_reason FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? AND verification_selected = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date',
      )
      .all(executionId)
      .map((r) => ({ ...toCellKey(r), reason: r.selection_reason }));
  }

  resumable(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, manual: boolean): CellKey[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CellKeyDbRow>(RESUMABLE_SQL).all({ executionId, stage, providerId, manual: manual ? 1 : 0 }).map(toCellKey);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1: `not_run` and `skipped` outcomes of cells departing on or before `today` become `skipped:departed`; priced/empty/no_data untouched. */
  markDeparted(executionId: string, today: IsoDate): number {
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = 'skipped', reason = 'departed'
           WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND departure_date <= @today
             AND (status = 'not_run' OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason <> 'departed'))`,
        )
        .run({ executionId, today }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) {
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery');
        this.recompute(executionId, 'verification');
      }
      return changes;
    });
  }
```

```ts
import type { NormalisedItinerary } from '@gmt/core';
import type { ObservationInsert } from '../../src/types.js';

/** A nonstop round trip on the worked example; `hash` stands in for WP2's sha256 hex. */
export function itinerary(hash = 'a'.repeat(64), c: CellKey = cell(), flight = 117): NormalisedItinerary {
  return {
    hash, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate, nights: 14,
    outbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight, from: c.origin, to: c.destination, departLocal: `${c.departureDate}T08:25`, arriveLocal: `${c.departureDate}T11:20`, durationMinutes: 475 }],
    inbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight - 5, from: c.destination, to: c.origin, departLocal: `${c.returnDate}T18:30`, arriveLocal: `${c.returnDate}T06:35`, durationMinutes: 425 }],
    outboundMinutes: 475, inboundMinutes: 425, stopsOut: 0, stopsIn: 0, layovers: [], airportChange: false, marketingCarriers: ['BA'],
  };
}

export function observation(executionId: string, over: Partial<ObservationInsert> = {}): ObservationInsert {
  return {
    executionId,
    provenance: { providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 0, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'GB', cabin: 'economy' },
    pricePence: 41_200, priceNativeMinor: 41_200, currencyNative: 'GBP',
    taxesIncluded: true, selfTransfer: false, segmentCabins: ['economy', 'economy'], baggage: { cabinBag: 'included', checkedBags: 1 }, fareConditions: null,
    booking: { kind: 'post', url: 'https://www.google.com/travel/clk/f', postData: 'u=<booking_request:0>', host: 'www.google.com' }, bookingExpiresAt: null,
    suppliedFields: ['segments', 'segmentTimes', 'segmentCabins', 'price', 'seller', 'baggage'], partialExpansion: false,
    ...over,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cellKeyOf } from '../src/internal/cellKey.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ItinerariesRepo } from '../src/repositories/itineraries.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, itinerary, newExecution, observation, snapshotOf } from './helpers/factories.js';

const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;

function setup(syntheticAllowed = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, repo: new ItinerariesRepo(db, clock, { syntheticAllowed }), cells: new CellsRepo(db, clock, { syntheticAllowed }) };
}
const offerOf = (hash = 'a'.repeat(64), seller = 'British Airways') => ({ itineraryHash: hash, providerId: 'searchapi' as const, seller, cabin: 'economy' as const });
const n = (db: ReturnType<typeof freshDb>, t: string) => (db.raw.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ${t}`).get() as { n: number }).n;

describe('itineraries and offers (spec §7)', () => {
  it('itineraries and segments are insert-or-ignore on hash', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    const it = itinerary();
    repo.upsertItinerary(it, { outbound: it.outbound, inbound: it.inbound });
    repo.upsertItinerary({ ...it, nights: 99 }, { outbound: it.outbound, inbound: it.inbound });
    expect(n(db, 'itineraries')).toBe(1);
    expect(n(db, 'segments')).toBe(2);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT nights FROM itineraries').get()).toEqual({ nights: 14 });
  });

  it('UNIQUE (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin): upsertOffer returns the same id; a raw duplicate throws', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    const it = itinerary();
    repo.upsertItinerary(it, { outbound: it.outbound, inbound: it.inbound });
    const id = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf())).toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf(undefined, 'Mytrip'))).not.toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), cabin: 'business' })).not.toBe(id);
    let code: string | null = null;
    try {
      db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (?, 'searchapi', 'British Airways', 'economy', ?, ?)`).run(it.hash, T0, T0);
    } catch (e) {
      code = e instanceof Database.SqliteError ? e.code : null;
    }
    expect(code).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
  });

  it("latest = max fetched_at, ties broken by id; first, lowest and highest follow", () => {
    const { db, executionId, repo } = setup();
    const it = itinerary();
    repo.upsertItinerary(it, { outbound: it.outbound, inbound: it.inbound });
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const obs = (fetchedAt: string, pence: number) =>
      repo.insertObservation({ ...observation(executionId, { pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence, provenance: { ...observation(executionId).provenance, fetchedAt: at(fetchedAt) } }), offerId });
    obs('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', 50_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 45_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z', 40_000); // inserted later, fetched earlier
    const read = () => db.raw.prepare('SELECT first_price_pence AS f, lowest_price_pence AS l, highest_price_pence AS h, latest_price_pence AS x, first_seen_at AS fs, last_seen_at AS ls FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(offerId);
    expect(read()).toEqual({ f: 50_000, l: 40_000, h: 50_000, x: 45_000, fs: '2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', ls: '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z' });
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 47_000); // same fetched_at as the latest: the higher id wins
    expect(read()).toMatchObject({ x: 47_000 });
  });

  it('observations reject provider ⇔ kind violations, the synthetic gate, a cabin or provider that disagrees with the offer, and non-integers', () => {
    const { executionId, repo } = setup(false);
    const it = itinerary();
    repo.upsertItinerary(it, { outbound: it.outbound, inbound: it.inbound });
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const base = observation(executionId);
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof observation>[1]) => () => repo.insertObservation({ ...observation(executionId, over), offerId });
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base.provenance, sourceKind: 'synthetic' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base.provenance, cabin: 'business' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base.provenance, providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write({ pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base.provenance, fetchedAt: at('2026-10-14T12:00:00+00:00') } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'timestamp' }));
    const fixtureOffer = repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), providerId: 'fixture' });
    expect(() => repo.insertObservation({ ...observation(executionId, { provenance: { ...base.provenance, providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' } }), offerId: fixtureOffer })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }),
    );
  });
});

describe('CellsRepo.writeVerifiedCell (spec §7 atomic unit "each verified cell")', () => {
  function seeded(syntheticAllowed = false) {
    const s = setup(syntheticAllowed);
    s.cells.seed(s.executionId, [cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)], []);
    s.cells.seedVerification(s.executionId, [{ ...cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], ['searchapi']);
    return s;
  }
  const write = (executionId: string, offers: Array<{ hash: string; seller: string; pence: number }>) => ({
    status: 'priced' as const, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy' as const, pointOfSale: 'GB' as const, sourceKind: 'scraped-search' as const, countAttempt: false,
    offers: offers.map((o) => ({ itinerary: itinerary(o.hash), offer: offerOf(o.hash, o.seller), observation: observation(executionId, { pricePence: o.pence, priceNativeMinor: o.pence }) })),
  });

  it('writes itinerary, segments, offer, observation, outcome, best_observation_id and roll-up together', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), write(executionId, [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]));
    expect([n(db, 'itineraries'), n(db, 'segments'), n(db, 'fare_offers'), n(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([1, 2, 2, 2]);
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyOf(cell()))?.verification).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 57_300 });
    const best = db.raw.prepare('SELECT o.price_pence AS p FROM search_cells c JOIN price_observations o ON o.id = c.best_observation_id WHERE c.origin = ? AND c.destination = ?').get('LHR', 'JFK');
    expect(best).toEqual({ p: 57_300 });
  });

  it('a failure on the second offer leaves no itinerary, offer, observation or outcome change', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    const w = write(executionId, [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'b'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]);
    const second = w.offers[1];
    if (second === undefined) throw new Error('fixture has two offers');
    second.observation = { ...second.observation, pricePence: 573.5, priceNativeMinor: 573.5 };
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), w)).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect([n(db, 'itineraries'), n(db, 'segments'), n(db, 'fare_offers'), n(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0]);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cell: cell() })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
  });

  it('rejects priced without offers, offers on a non-priced outcome, and an itinerary for another cell', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = seeded();
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), write(executionId, []))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'priced-needs-offer' }));
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), { ...write(executionId, [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 1 }]), status: 'empty' })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offers-need-priced' }),
    );
    const w = write(executionId, [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 1 }]);
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(undefined, EWR), w)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'cell-mismatch' }));
  });

  it('an empty verified cell records the outcome with no rows', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), { ...write(executionId, []), status: 'empty' });
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyOf(cell()))?.verification.status).toBe('empty');
    expect(n(db, 'price_observations')).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('ItinerariesRepo reads', () => {
  it('observationsForDefinition filters by execution set, current space and cabin, and includes children', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId, cells, repo } = setup();
    cells.seed(executionId, [cell()], []);
    cells.seedVerification(executionId, [{ ...cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], ['searchapi']);
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', cell(), {
      status: 'priced', reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', countAttempt: false,
      offers: [{ itinerary: itinerary(), offer: offerOf(), observation: observation(executionId) }],
    });
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    new CellsRepo(db, clock).seed(child.id, [cell(undefined, EWR)], []);
    new CellsRepo(db, clock).seedVerification(child.id, [{ ...cell(undefined, EWR), reason: 'always_include' }], ['searchapi']);
    new CellsRepo(db, clock).writeVerifiedCell(child.id, 'searchapi', cell(undefined, EWR), {
      status: 'priced', reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', countAttempt: false,
      offers: [{ itinerary: itinerary('c'.repeat(64), cell(undefined, EWR)), offer: offerOf('c'.repeat(64)), observation: observation(child.id, { pricePence: 39_000, priceNativeMinor: 39_000 }) }],
    });
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    const rows = repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, [], space, 'economy');
    expect(rows.map((r) => [r.executionId, r.pricePence, r.cell.destination])).toEqual([[child.id, 39_000, 'EWR'], [executionId, 41_200, 'JFK']]);
    expect(rows[0]?.provenance).toEqual({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 0, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'GB', cabin: 'economy' });
    expect(rows[0]?.booking).toEqual({ kind: 'post', url: 'https://www.google.com/travel/clk/f', postData: 'u=<booking_request:0>', host: 'www.google.com' });
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, [executionId], space, 'economy')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, [], space, 'business')).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, [], { ...space, maxNights: 13 }, 'economy')).toHaveLength(0); // 14-night cells fall outside
    expect(repo.offerHistory('a'.repeat(64))).toEqual([{ fetchedAt: T0, date: null, pricePence: 41_200, providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', cabin: 'economy', seller: 'British Airways', executionId }]);
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell())).toMatchObject({ executionId, status: 'priced' });
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-01-01', '2027-01-15'))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `itineraries`: cabin-independent flights, identity by canonical hash (spec §6.4). Insert-or-ignore. */
export const itineraries = sqliteTable(
  'itineraries',
  {
    hash: text('hash').primaryKey(),
    kind: text('kind').notNull().default('flight'),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    nights: integer('nights').notNull(),
    outboundMinutes: integer('outbound_minutes'),
    inboundMinutes: integer('inbound_minutes'),
    stopsOut: integer('stops_out').notNull(),
    stopsIn: integer('stops_in').notNull(),
    airportChange: integer('airport_change'),
    carriersJson: text('carriers_json').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('itineraries_nights_ck', isInteger('nights')),
    check('itineraries_stops_out_ck', isInteger('stops_out')),
    check('itineraries_stops_in_ck', isInteger('stops_in')),
    check('itineraries_out_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('outbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_in_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('inbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_airport_change_ck', isBool('airport_change', true)),
    check('itineraries_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `segments`: no cabin (segments belong to the cabin-independent itinerary). */
export const segments = sqliteTable(
  'segments',
  {
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    direction: text('direction').notNull(),
    position: integer('position').notNull(),
    mode: text('mode').notNull().default('air'),
    marketingCarrier: text('marketing_carrier').notNull(),
    operatingCarrier: text('operating_carrier'),
    flightNumber: integer('flight_number').notNull(),
    fromIata: text('from_iata').notNull(),
    toIata: text('to_iata').notNull(),
    departLocal: text('depart_local').notNull(),
    arriveLocal: text('arrive_local').notNull(),
    departUtc: text('depart_utc'),
    arriveUtc: text('arrive_utc'),
    durationMinutes: integer('duration_minutes'),
    aircraft: text('aircraft'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.itineraryHash, t.direction, t.position] }),
    check('segments_direction_ck', isOneOf('direction', ['outbound', 'inbound'])),
    check('segments_position_ck', isInteger('position')),
    check('segments_flight_number_ck', isInteger('flight_number')),
    check('segments_duration_ck', isNullOrInteger('duration_minutes')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS, isIsoTimestamp, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `fare_offers`: (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin). Price aggregates are recomputed from observations. */
export const fareOffers = sqliteTable(
  'fare_offers',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    seller: text('seller').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
    lastSeenAt: text('last_seen_at').notNull(),
    firstPricePence: integer('first_price_pence'),
    lowestPricePence: integer('lowest_price_pence'),
    highestPricePence: integer('highest_price_pence'),
    latestPricePence: integer('latest_price_pence'),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('fare_offers_identity_uq').on(t.itineraryHash, t.providerId, t.seller, t.cabin),
    index('fare_offers_itinerary_idx').on(t.itineraryHash),
    check('fare_offers_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('fare_offers_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check('fare_offers_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_last_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('last_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_first_ck', isNullOrInteger('first_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_lowest_ck', isNullOrInteger('lowest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_highest_ck', isNullOrInteger('highest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_latest_ck', isNullOrInteger('latest_price_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/priceObservations.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { fareOffers } from './fareOffers.js';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `price_observations`: append-only; verdicts are recomputed at read time from `supplied_fields_json`. */
export const priceObservations = sqliteTable(
  'price_observations',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    offerId: integer('offer_id').notNull().references(() => fareOffers.id),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    taxesIncluded: integer('taxes_included'),
    selfTransfer: integer('self_transfer'),
    segmentCabinsJson: text('segment_cabins_json'),
    baggageJson: text('baggage_json'),
    fareConditionsJson: text('fare_conditions_json'),
    bookingJson: text('booking_json'),
    bookingExpiresAt: text('booking_expires_at'),
    suppliedFieldsJson: text('supplied_fields_json').notNull(),
    partialExpansion: integer('partial_expansion').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('price_observations_execution_price_idx').on(t.executionId, t.pricePence),
    index('price_observations_offer_fetched_idx').on(t.offerId, t.fetchedAt),
    ...priceTableChecks('price_observations'),
    check('price_observations_taxes_ck', isBool('taxes_included', true)),
    check('price_observations_self_transfer_ck', isBool('self_transfer', true)),
    check('price_observations_partial_ck', isBool('partial_expansion')),
    check('price_observations_expires_ck', isIsoTimestamp('booking_expires_at', true)),
  ],
);
```
