`packages/db/src/repositories/itineraries.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  BaggageSnapshot, Booking, Cabin, CellKey, CellOutcome, Clock, FareConditions, FxInfo, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, NormalisedItinerary,
  Offer, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, ResolvedSpace, Segment, SourceKind, SuppliedField,
} from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { HistoryPoint, ObservationInsert, ObservationRow } from '../types.js';
import { type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';

interface ObservationDbRow {
  id: number; offer_id: number; execution_id: string; source_kind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null;
  price_pence: number; price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null;
  fx_converted: number; point_of_sale: PointOfSale; max_age_hours: number | null; taxes_included: number | null; self_transfer: number | null;
  segment_cabins_json: string | null; baggage_json: string | null; fare_conditions_json: string | null; booking_json: string | null;
  booking_expires_at: string | null; supplied_fields_json: string; partial_expansion: number;
  // joined
  itinerary_hash: string; provider_id: ProviderId; seller: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string;
}

const nullableBool = (v: number | null): boolean | null => (v === null ? null : v === 1);
const parseOr = <T>(json: string | null, fallback: T): T => (json === null ? fallback : (JSON.parse(json) as T));

function toObservationRow(r: ObservationDbRow): ObservationRow {
  let fx: FxInfo = { converted: false };
  if (r.fx_converted === 1) {
    if (r.fx_rate_nano === null || r.fx_rate_date === null || r.fx_source === null) throw new Error(`observation ${r.id} is converted without rate data`);
    fx = { converted: true, rateNano: r.fx_rate_nano, rateDate: r.fx_rate_date as IsoDate, source: r.fx_source };
  }
  const provenance: Provenance = {
    providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime,
    ...(r.provider_observed_at === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt: r.provider_observed_at as IsoDateTime }),
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin: r.cabin,
  };
  return {
    id: r.id, offerId: r.offer_id, executionId: r.execution_id, provenance, pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native,
    taxesIncluded: nullableBool(r.taxes_included), selfTransfer: nullableBool(r.self_transfer),
    segmentCabins: parseOr<Cabin[] | null>(r.segment_cabins_json, null), baggage: parseOr<BaggageSnapshot | null>(r.baggage_json, null),
    fareConditions: parseOr<FareConditions | null>(r.fare_conditions_json, null), booking: parseOr<Booking>(r.booking_json, { kind: 'none' }),
    bookingExpiresAt: r.booking_expires_at as IsoDateTime | null, suppliedFields: JSON.parse(r.supplied_fields_json) as SuppliedField[], partialExpansion: r.partial_expansion === 1,
    offer: { itineraryHash: r.itinerary_hash, providerId: r.provider_id, seller: r.seller, cabin: r.cabin },
    cell: { origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate },
  };
}

const OBSERVATION_JOIN = `
FROM price_observations o
JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id
JOIN itineraries i ON i.hash = f.itinerary_hash
JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id`;
const OBSERVATION_COLUMNS = 'o.*, f.itinerary_hash, f.provider_id, f.seller, i.origin, i.destination, i.departure_date, i.return_date';

const RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL = `
UPDATE fare_offers SET
  first_seen_at = (SELECT MIN(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  last_seen_at = (SELECT MAX(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  first_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1),
  lowest_price_pence = (SELECT MIN(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  highest_price_pence = (SELECT MAX(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  latest_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)
WHERE id = @id`;

/** Spec §7 itineraries, segments, fare offers and price observations. */
export class ItinerariesRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: { syntheticAllowed: boolean } = { syntheticAllowed: false },
  ) {}

  /** Insert-or-ignore on hash, with its segments, in one transaction (spec §7). */
  upsertItinerary(it: NormalisedItinerary, segments: { outbound: readonly Segment[]; inbound: readonly Segment[] }): void {
    if (it.hash.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('hash', 'itinerary hash is empty');
    assertInt('nights', it.nights);
    assertInt('stops_out', it.stopsOut);
    assertInt('stops_in', it.stopsIn);
    assertNullableInt('outbound_minutes', it.outboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertNullableInt('inbound_minutes', it.inboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertIsoDate('departure_date', it.departureDate);
    assertIsoDate('return_date', it.returnDate);
    const segRows = [
      ...segments.outbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'outbound', position })),
      ...segments.inbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'inbound', position })),
    ];
    for (const { s } of segRows) {
      assertInt('flight_number', s.flightNumber);
      assertNullableInt('duration_minutes', s.durationMinutes ?? null);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO itineraries (hash, kind, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, nights, outbound_minutes, inbound_minutes, stops_out, stops_in, airport_change, carriers_json, first_seen_at)
           VALUES (@hash, 'flight', @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @nights, @outboundMinutes, @inboundMinutes, @stopsOut, @stopsIn, @airportChange, @carriers, @now)
           ON CONFLICT(hash) DO NOTHING`,
        )
        .run({
          hash: it.hash, origin: it.origin, destination: it.destination, departureDate: it.departureDate, returnDate: it.returnDate, nights: it.nights,
          outboundMinutes: it.outboundMinutes ?? null, inboundMinutes: it.inboundMinutes ?? null, stopsOut: it.stopsOut, stopsIn: it.stopsIn,
          airportChange: it.airportChange === undefined ? null : it.airportChange ? 1 : 0, carriers: JSON.stringify(it.marketingCarriers), now: this.clock.now(),
        });
      const insertSegment = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO segments (itinerary_hash, direction, position, mode, marketing_carrier, operating_carrier, flight_number, from_iata, to_iata, depart_local, arrive_local, depart_utc, arrive_utc, duration_minutes, aircraft)
         VALUES (@hash, @direction, @position, 'air', @marketingCarrier, @operatingCarrier, @flightNumber, @from, @to, @departLocal, @arriveLocal, @departUtc, @arriveUtc, @durationMinutes, @aircraft)
         ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, direction, position) DO NOTHING`,
      );
      for (const { s, direction, position } of segRows) {
        insertSegment.run({
          hash: it.hash, direction, position, marketingCarrier: s.marketingCarrier, operatingCarrier: s.operatingCarrier ?? null, flightNumber: s.flightNumber,
          from: s.from, to: s.to, departLocal: s.departLocal, arriveLocal: s.arriveLocal, departUtc: s.departUtc ?? null, arriveUtc: s.arriveUtc ?? null,
          durationMinutes: s.durationMinutes ?? null, aircraft: s.aircraft ?? null,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  /** Returns the offer id for (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin), creating it if absent. */
  upsertOffer(offer: Offer): number {
    if (offer.seller.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('seller', 'seller is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (@itineraryHash, @providerId, @seller, @cabin, @now, @now)
         ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin) DO NOTHING`,
      )
      .run({ ...offer, now });
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { id: number }>('SELECT id FROM fare_offers WHERE itinerary_hash = @itineraryHash AND provider_id = @providerId AND seller = @seller AND cabin = @cabin')
      .get({ ...offer });
    if (r === undefined) throw new Error('fare offer vanished after upsert');
    return r.id;
  }

  /** Appends one observation and recomputes the offer's first/lowest/highest/latest (latest = max fetched_at, tie id). */
  insertObservation(obs: ObservationInsert & { offerId: number }): number {
    const offer = this.db.raw.prepare<[number], { provider_id: ProviderId; cabin: Cabin }>('SELECT provider_id, cabin FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(obs.offerId);
    if (offer === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `fare offer ${obs.offerId} does not exist`);
    const p = obs.provenance;
    assertProviderKind(p.providerId, p.sourceKind, this.opts.syntheticAllowed);
    if (p.providerId !== offer.provider_id || p.cabin !== offer.cabin) {
      throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `observation ${p.providerId}/${p.cabin} does not match offer ${offer.provider_id}/${offer.cabin}`);
    }
    assertPriceMoney({ pricePence: obs.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: obs.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: obs.currencyNative, fx: p.fx });
    assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', p.fetchedAt);
    if (p.providerObservedAt !== undefined) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', p.providerObservedAt);
    assertNullableIsoDateTime('booking_expires_at', obs.bookingExpiresAt);
    if (p.maxAgeHours !== 'unknown') assertInt('max_age_hours', p.maxAgeHours);
    const fx = p.fx;
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const id = Number(
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO price_observations (offer_id, execution_id, source_kind, cabin, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, price_pence, price_native_minor, currency_native,
              fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, max_age_hours, taxes_included, self_transfer, segment_cabins_json, baggage_json,
              fare_conditions_json, booking_json, booking_expires_at, supplied_fields_json, partial_expansion)
             VALUES (@offerId, @executionId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor, @currencyNative,
              @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @maxAgeHours, @taxesIncluded, @selfTransfer, @segmentCabins, @baggage,
              @fareConditions, @booking, @bookingExpiresAt, @suppliedFields, @partialExpansion)`,
          )
          .run({
            offerId: obs.offerId, executionId: obs.executionId, sourceKind: p.sourceKind, cabin: p.cabin, fetchedAt: p.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: p.providerObservedAt ?? null,
            pricePence: obs.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: obs.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: obs.currencyNative,
            fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
            pointOfSale: p.pointOfSale,
            // why: spec §7 "max_age_hours NULL (NULL = unknown; written explicitly from CapabilityInfo.maxAgeHours)".
            maxAgeHours: p.maxAgeHours === 'unknown' ? null : p.maxAgeHours,
            taxesIncluded: obs.taxesIncluded === null ? null : obs.taxesIncluded ? 1 : 0, selfTransfer: obs.selfTransfer === null ? null : obs.selfTransfer ? 1 : 0,
            segmentCabins: obs.segmentCabins === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.segmentCabins), baggage: obs.baggage === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.baggage),
            fareConditions: obs.fareConditions === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.fareConditions), booking: JSON.stringify(obs.booking),
            bookingExpiresAt: obs.bookingExpiresAt, suppliedFields: JSON.stringify(obs.suppliedFields), partialExpansion: obs.partialExpansion ? 1 : 0,
          }).lastInsertRowid,
      );
      this.db.raw.prepare(RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL).run({ id: obs.offerId });
      return id;
    });
  }

  /**
   * Observations from this definition's executions, children included (spec §8.1), restricted to the
   * current resolved space and cabin (spec §6.2 "Rows whose cell is outside … are excluded").
   * An empty `executionIds` means every execution of the definition. Cheapest first.
   */
  observationsForDefinition(definitionId: string, executionIds: readonly string[], space: ResolvedSpace, cabin: Cabin): ObservationRow[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ObservationDbRow>(
        `SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN}
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId
           AND (json_array_length(@executionIds) = 0 OR o.execution_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@executionIds)))
           AND f.cabin = @cabin
           AND i.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND i.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
           AND i.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND i.nights BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights
         ORDER BY o.price_pence, o.id`,
      )
      .all({
        definitionId, executionIds: JSON.stringify(executionIds), cabin, origins: JSON.stringify(space.origins), destinations: JSON.stringify(space.destinations),
        from: space.departureFrom, to: space.departureTo, minNights: space.minNights, maxNights: space.maxNights,
      })
      .map(toObservationRow);
  }

  /** Every observation of every offer of one itinerary, oldest first (History view, spec §10). */
  offerHistory(hash: string): HistoryPoint[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], ObservationDbRow>(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN} WHERE f.itinerary_hash = ? ORDER BY o.fetched_at, o.id`)
      .all(hash)
      .map((r) => ({ fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime, date: null, pricePence: r.price_pence, providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, cabin: r.cabin, seller: r.seller, executionId: r.execution_id }));
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "not seen in latest search": the newest priced|empty verification outcome for the cell across this definition's executions. */
  latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey): CellOutcome | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT o.* FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND o.stage = 'verification' AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty')
           AND o.origin = @origin AND o.destination = @destination AND o.departure_date = @departureDate AND o.return_date = @returnDate
         ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.started_at DESC, e.id DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toOutcome(r);
  }
}
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one verified cell's itinerary, segments, offer, observation, outcome,
   * `best_observation_id` and roll-up. A cell is `priced` only if ≥ 1 offer is written (spec §5.4).
   */
  writeVerifiedCell(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cell: CellKey, write: VerifiedCellWrite): void {
    if (write.status === 'priced' && write.offers.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-offer', `${cellKeyOf(cell)} is priced with no offer (spec §5.4)`);
    if (write.status !== 'priced' && write.offers.length > 0) throw new RowRejected('offers-need-priced', `${cellKeyOf(cell)} has offers but status ${write.status}`);
    const key = cellKeyOf(cell);
    for (const o of write.offers) {
      if (cellKeyOf(o.itinerary) !== key) throw new RowRejected('cell-mismatch', `itinerary ${o.itinerary.hash} is for ${cellKeyOf(o.itinerary)}, not ${key}`);
      if (o.offer.itineraryHash !== o.itinerary.hash || o.offer.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `offer does not belong to itinerary ${o.itinerary.hash} from ${providerId}`);
      if (o.observation.executionId !== executionId) throw new RowRejected('execution-mismatch', `observation for ${o.observation.executionId} written to ${executionId}`);
    }
    assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', write.fetchedAt);
    const itineraries = new ItinerariesRepo(this.db, this.clock, this.opts);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      let best: { id: number; pence: number } | null = null;
      for (const o of write.offers) {
        itineraries.upsertItinerary(o.itinerary, { outbound: o.itinerary.outbound, inbound: o.itinerary.inbound });
        const offerId = itineraries.upsertOffer(o.offer);
        const id = itineraries.insertObservation({ ...o.observation, offerId });
        if (best === null || o.observation.pricePence < best.pence) best = { id, pence: o.observation.pricePence };
      }
      const params = { executionId, providerId, origin: cell.origin, destination: cell.destination, departureDate: cell.departureDate, returnDate: cell.returnDate };
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, last_error = @lastError, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence,
             cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, attempts = attempts + @attempt
           WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = 'verification' AND provider_id = @providerId`,
        )
        .run({
          ...params, status: write.status, reason: write.reason, lastError: write.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(write.lastError),
          fetchedAt: write.fetchedAt, minPence: best?.pence ?? null, cabin: write.cabin, pointOfSale: write.pointOfSale, sourceKind: write.sourceKind,
          attempt: write.countAttempt ? 1 : 0,
        }).changes;
      if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded verification outcome for ${providerId} at ${key}`);
      if (best !== null) {
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(
            `UPDATE search_cells SET best_observation_id = @bestId WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}
               AND (best_observation_id IS NULL OR (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE id = search_cells.best_observation_id) > @bestPence)`,
          )
          .run({ ...params, bestId: best.id, bestPence: best.pence });
      }
      this.recompute(executionId, 'verification', cell);
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }
```

```ts
const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;

const E = cell(undefined, EWR); // empty
const N = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'); // no_data
const X = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-16', '2027-03-30'); // error
const R = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-17', '2027-03-31'); // left not_run
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/calendarPrices.ts` (imports: `CellKey`, `CellOutcome`, `ResolvedSpace` from `@gmt/core`; `cellKeyOf` from `../internal/cellKey.js`; `type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome` from `../internal/outcomeRow.js`; `rollUpUpdateSql` from `../internal/rollUpSql.js`; `HistoryPoint` from `../types.js`):

```ts
/**
 * Spec §8.1 reuse source: per cell, the newest `priced | empty | no_data` discovery outcome for the
 * same definition, provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind, from a non-child execution, whose
 * original `fetched_at` is newer than the cutoff (the engine passes now − 0.9 × discoveryRefreshHours).
 */
const REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT o.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
           PARTITION BY o.origin, o.destination, o.departure_date, o.return_date
           ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.started_at DESC, o.execution_id DESC) AS rn
  FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND e.is_child = 0
    AND o.stage = 'discovery' AND o.provider_id = @providerId
    AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty', 'no_data')
    AND o.cabin = @cabin AND o.point_of_sale = @pointOfSale AND o.source_kind = @sourceKind
    AND o.fetched_at > @newerThan
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1`;

const PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL = `
SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId
  AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate`;

/** Spec §6.9 calendar basis: latest fetched per (provider, cell) in the cabin, reused copies ignored, inside the current space. */
const LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT p.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
           PARTITION BY p.provider_id, p.origin, p.destination, p.departure_date, p.return_date
           ORDER BY p.fetched_at DESC, p.id DESC) AS rn
  FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL
    AND p.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND p.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
    AND p.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to
    AND CAST(julianday(p.return_date) - julianday(p.departure_date) AS INTEGER) BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, provider_id`;
```

Methods on `CalendarPricesRepo`:

```ts
  reuseCandidates(
    definitionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cabin: Cabin, pointOfSale: PointOfSale, sourceKind: SourceKind,
    cells: readonly CellKey[], newerThan: IsoDateTime,
  ): Map<string, { outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }> {
    assertIsoDateTime('newerThan', newerThan);
    const wanted = new Set(cells.map(cellKeyOf));
    const priceStmt = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL);
    const out = new Map<string, { outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }>();
    const rows = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL).all({ definitionId, providerId, cabin, pointOfSale, sourceKind, newerThan });
    for (const r of rows) {
      const outcome = toOutcome(r);
      const key = cellKeyOf(outcome);
      if (!wanted.has(key)) continue;
      let price: CalendarPriceRow | null = null;
      if (outcome.status === 'priced') {
        const p = priceStmt.get({ executionId: outcome.executionId, providerId, origin: outcome.origin, destination: outcome.destination, departureDate: outcome.departureDate, returnDate: outcome.returnDate });
        // why: a priced outcome always has its price row (writeTile enforces it); a missing one is corruption, not a reusable cell.
        if (p === undefined) throw new Error(`priced outcome ${key} in ${outcome.executionId} has no calendar price`);
        price = toCalendarPriceRow(p);
      }
      out.set(key, { outcome, price });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "each reused cell's outcome + copied prices + roll-up" (one transaction for the
   * batch). Copies keep the original `fetched_at` and provenance; `reused_from_execution_id` is always
   * the execution that fetched the price, even when the source is itself a reused copy.
   */
  copyReused(executionId: string, rows: ReadonlyArray<{ outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }>): void {
    for (const { outcome, price } of rows) {
      if (outcome.status !== 'priced' && outcome.status !== 'empty' && outcome.status !== 'no_data') {
        throw new RowRejected('reuse-source', `only priced, empty or no_data outcomes are reused, got ${outcome.status} (spec §8.1)`);
      }
      if ((outcome.status === 'priced') !== (price !== null)) throw new RowRejected('reuse-source', `reused ${outcome.status} outcome with${price === null ? 'out' : ''} a price`);
    }
    const select = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { status: string }>(
      `SELECT status FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination
         AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate AND stage = 'discovery' AND provider_id = @providerId`,
    );
    const update = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence, cabin = @cabin,
         point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFrom
       WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate
         AND return_date = @returnDate AND stage = 'discovery' AND provider_id = @providerId`,
    );
    const recompute = this.db.raw.prepare(rollUpUpdateSql('discovery', 'cell'));
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const { outcome, price } of rows) {
        const key = { executionId, providerId: outcome.providerId, origin: outcome.origin, destination: outcome.destination, departureDate: outcome.departureDate, returnDate: outcome.returnDate };
        const target = select.get(key);
        if (target?.status !== 'not_run') throw new RowRejected('reuse-target', `${cellKeyOf(outcome)} in ${executionId} is ${target?.status ?? 'missing'}, not not_run`);
        const reusedFrom = outcome.reusedFromExecutionId ?? outcome.executionId;
        update.run({ ...key, status: outcome.status, reason: outcome.reason, fetchedAt: outcome.fetchedAt, minPence: outcome.minPence, cabin: outcome.cabin, pointOfSale: outcome.pointOfSale, sourceKind: outcome.sourceKind, reusedFrom });
        if (price !== null) {
          const copy = { ...price, executionId, reusedFromExecutionId: reusedFrom };
          validateCalendarPrice(copy, this.opts.syntheticAllowed);
          insertCalendarPrice(this.db, copy);
        }
        recompute.run({ executionId, origin: outcome.origin, destination: outcome.destination, departureDate: outcome.departureDate, returnDate: outcome.returnDate });
      }
    });
  }

  /** Spec §7 "a fresh price superseding a reused row deletes that row in the same transaction". Fresh rows are never deleted. */
  supersedeReused(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cell: CellKey): void {
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination
           AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`,
      )
      .run({ executionId, providerId, origin: cell.origin, destination: cell.destination, departureDate: cell.departureDate, returnDate: cell.returnDate });
  }

  latestPerCell(definitionId: string, cabin: Cabin, space: ResolvedSpace): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL)
      .all({
        definitionId, cabin, origins: JSON.stringify(space.origins), destinations: JSON.stringify(space.destinations),
        from: space.departureFrom, to: space.departureTo, minNights: space.minNights, maxNights: space.maxNights,
      })
      .map(toCalendarPriceRow);
  }

  /** Route calendar history, fetched rows only (reused copies excluded), oldest first. `date` is the cell's departure date. */
  history(origin: Iata, destination: Iata, cabin: Cabin): HistoryPoint[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND cabin = @cabin AND reused_from_execution_id IS NULL ORDER BY fetched_at, id`,
      )
      .all({ origin, destination, cabin })
      .map((r) => ({ fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime, date: r.departure_date as IsoDate, pricePence: r.price_pence, providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, cabin: r.cabin, seller: null, executionId: r.execution_id }));
  }
```

```ts
}

describe('AlertsRepo (spec §6.8, §7 alert_rules / alert_events)', () => {

    const r = alerts.create(rule);

    expect(alerts.update(r.id, { thresholdPence: 40_000, enabled: false })).toMatchObject({ thresholdPence: 40_000, enabled: false });
    expect(alerts.rules()).toHaveLength(1);
    db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO alert_rules (id, definition_id, type, basis, enabled) VALUES ('r2', ?, 'dropPct', 'calendar', 1)`).run(definition.id);
    expect(alerts.rules(definition.id).find((x) => x.id === 'r2')?.cooldownHours).toBe(336);
    alerts.delete(r.id);
    expect(alerts.rules(definition.id).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['r2']);

    const { rule, alerts } = setup();

    expect(() => alerts.create({ ...rule, thresholdPence: 450.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));

    alerts.insertEvent({ ...base, firedAt: at('2026-10-01T05:00:00.000Z'), status: 'sent', payload: { link: 'https://www.google.com/travel/clk?token=SECRET' } });
    alerts.insertEvent({ ...base, firedAt: at('2026-10-10T05:00:00.000Z'), status: 'failed', payload: {} });
    expect(alerts.lastFiring(r.id, { dedupKey: 'r|hash-a|412' }, at('2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z'))).toMatchObject({ firedAt: '2026-10-01T05:00:00.000Z', status: 'sent' });
    expect(alerts.lastFiring(r.id, { identityKey: 'r|hash-a' }, at('2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z'))?.firedAt).toBe('2026-10-01T05:00:00.000Z');
    expect(alerts.lastFiring(r.id, { dedupKey: 'r|hash-a|412' }, at('2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z'))).toBeNull();
    const [first] = alerts.events({ ruleId: r.id, limit: 10 }).slice(-1);
    expect(JSON.stringify(first?.payload)).not.toContain('SECRET');

describe('NotificationsRepo (spec §8.1 "deduplicated per execution and kind")', () => {
  it('records and answers wasSent per (kind, execution), including execution-less kinds', () => {
    const { db, clock } = setup();
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const n = new NotificationsRepo(db, clock);
    expect(n.wasSent('paused_budget', execution.id)).toBe(false);
    n.record('paused_budget', execution.id, 'sent');
    expect(n.wasSent('paused_budget', execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(n.wasSent('interrupted', execution.id)).toBe(false);
    n.record('skipped_missed', null, 'failed');
    expect(n.wasSent('skipped_missed', null)).toBe(false); // a failed send is not "sent"
    n.record('skipped_missed', null, 'sent');
    expect(n.wasSent('skipped_missed', null)).toBe(true);
  });
});

    expect(() => repo.upsert([row('2026-09-03', 1.5)])).toThrow(RowRejected);

});
```

```ts
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchDefinitions } from './searchDefinitions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `alert_rules`. `cooldown_hours` defaults to 336, the §6.8 rule value ("default 336,
 * deliberately not equal to the weekly cadence"); §7's column list says 168 (reported mismatch).
 */
export const alertRules = sqliteTable(
  'alert_rules',
  {
    id: text('id').primaryKey(),
    definitionId: text('definition_id').notNull().references(() => searchDefinitions.id),
    type: text('type').notNull(),
    thresholdPence: integer('threshold_pence'),
    pct: integer('pct'),
    basis: text('basis').notNull(),
    requireAllVerified: integer('require_all_verified'),
    cooldownHours: integer('cooldown_hours').notNull().default(336),
    enabled: integer('enabled').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('alert_rules_type_ck', isOneOf('type', ['absolute', 'dropPct', 'dropAbs'])),
    check('alert_rules_basis_ck', isOneOf('basis', ['verified', 'calendar'])),
    check('alert_rules_threshold_ck', isNullOrInteger('threshold_pence')),
    check('alert_rules_pct_ck', isNullOrInteger('pct')),
    check('alert_rules_cooldown_ck', isInteger('cooldown_hours')),
    check('alert_rules_require_ck', isBool('require_all_verified', true)),
    check('alert_rules_enabled_ck', isBool('enabled')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { alertRules } from './alertRules.js';
import { calendarPrices } from './calendarPrices.js';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { priceObservations } from './priceObservations.js';

/** Spec §7 `alert_events`. Deleting a rule deletes its events. */
export const alertEvents = sqliteTable(
  'alert_events',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    ruleId: text('rule_id').notNull().references(() => alertRules.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    identityKey: text('identity_key').notNull(),
    observationId: integer('observation_id').references(() => priceObservations.id),
    calendarPriceId: integer('calendar_price_id').references(() => calendarPrices.id),
    dedupKey: text('dedup_key').notNull(),
    firedAt: text('fired_at').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    reason: text('reason'),
    payloadJson: text('payload_json').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('alert_events_rule_dedup_idx').on(t.ruleId, t.dedupKey, t.firedAt),
    index('alert_events_rule_identity_idx').on(t.ruleId, t.identityKey, t.firedAt),
    check('alert_events_status_ck', isOneOf('status', ['sent', 'failed', 'suppressed'])),
    check('alert_events_fired_ck', isIsoTimestamp('fired_at')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `operator_notifications`: dedup per execution and kind (spec §8.1). */
export const operatorNotifications = sqliteTable(
  'operator_notifications',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    kind: text('kind').notNull(),
    executionId: text('execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    sentAt: text('sent_at').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
  },

    index('operator_notifications_kind_execution_idx').on(t.kind, t.executionId),
    check('operator_notifications_status_ck', isOneOf('status', ['sent', 'failed'])),
    check('operator_notifications_sent_ck', isIsoTimestamp('sent_at')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `route_price_history`: Google's 60-day route lowest-price series from SearchApi step 1 (cabin unknown, labelled). */
export const routePriceHistory = sqliteTable(
  'route_price_history',
  {
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    date: text('date').notNull(),
    lowestPricePence: integer('lowest_price_pence').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.origin, t.destination, t.cabin, t.providerId, t.date] }),
    check('route_price_history_price_ck', isInteger('lowest_price_pence')),
    check('route_price_history_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check('route_price_history_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('route_price_history_fetched_ck', isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/alerts.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { type Clock, type IsoDateTime, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { AlertEvent, AlertEventFilter, AlertEventInput, AlertRule, AlertRuleInput } from '../types.js';

interface RuleDbRow { id: string; definition_id: string; type: AlertRule['type']; threshold_pence: number | null; pct: number | null; basis: AlertRule['basis']; require_all_verified: number | null; cooldown_hours: number; enabled: number }
interface EventDbRow { id: number; rule_id: string; identity_key: string; observation_id: number | null; calendar_price_id: number | null; dedup_key: string; fired_at: string; status: AlertEvent['status']; reason: string | null; payload_json: string }

const toRule = (r: RuleDbRow): AlertRule => ({
  id: r.id, definitionId: r.definition_id, type: r.type, thresholdPence: r.threshold_pence, pct: r.pct, basis: r.basis,
  requireAllVerified: r.require_all_verified === null ? null : r.require_all_verified === 1, cooldownHours: r.cooldown_hours, enabled: r.enabled === 1,
});
const toEvent = (r: EventDbRow): AlertEvent => ({
  id: r.id, ruleId: r.rule_id, identityKey: r.identity_key, observationId: r.observation_id, calendarPriceId: r.calendar_price_id, dedupKey: r.dedup_key,
  firedAt: r.fired_at as IsoDateTime, status: r.status, reason: r.reason, payload: JSON.parse(r.payload_json) as unknown,
});

function validateRule(r: AlertRuleInput): void {
  assertNullableInt('threshold_pence', r.thresholdPence);
  assertNullableInt('pct', r.pct);
  assertInt('cooldown_hours', r.cooldownHours);
  // why: spec §6.8 "requireAllVerified (verified basis only; zod rejects it on calendar basis)"; the repository holds the same line.
  if (r.basis === 'calendar' && r.requireAllVerified !== null) throw new RowRejected('require-all-verified', 'requireAllVerified applies to the verified basis only');
}

/** Spec §7 alert rules and events. Evaluation and dedup policy live in core (WP2) and the alert runner (WP11). */
export class AlertsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); event times come from the caller.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  rules(definitionId?: string): AlertRule[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, RuleDbRow>('SELECT * FROM alert_rules WHERE (@definitionId IS NULL OR definition_id = @definitionId) ORDER BY id')
      .all({ definitionId: definitionId ?? null })
      .map(toRule);
  }

  create(input: AlertRuleInput): AlertRule {
    validateRule(input);
    const id = randomUUID();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO alert_rules (id, definition_id, type, threshold_pence, pct, basis, require_all_verified, cooldown_hours, enabled)
         VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @type, @thresholdPence, @pct, @basis, @requireAllVerified, @cooldownHours, @enabled)`,
      )
      .run({ ...input, id, requireAllVerified: input.requireAllVerified === null ? null : input.requireAllVerified ? 1 : 0, enabled: input.enabled ? 1 : 0 });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  update(id: string, patch: Partial<AlertRuleInput>): AlertRule {
    const { id: _id, ...current } = this.mustGet(id);
    const merged: AlertRuleInput = { ...current, ...patch };
    validateRule(merged);
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `UPDATE alert_rules SET definition_id = @definitionId, type = @type, threshold_pence = @thresholdPence, pct = @pct, basis = @basis,
           require_all_verified = @requireAllVerified, cooldown_hours = @cooldownHours, enabled = @enabled WHERE id = @id`,
      )
      .run({ ...merged, id, requireAllVerified: merged.requireAllVerified === null ? null : merged.requireAllVerified ? 1 : 0, enabled: merged.enabled ? 1 : 0 });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  delete(id: string): void {
    this.db.raw.prepare('DELETE FROM alert_rules WHERE id = ?').run(id);
  }

  events(filter: AlertEventFilter): AlertEvent[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, EventDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM alert_events WHERE (@ruleId IS NULL OR rule_id = @ruleId) AND (@since IS NULL OR fired_at >= @since) ORDER BY fired_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT @limit`,
      )
      .all({ ruleId: filter.ruleId ?? null, since: filter.since ?? null, limit: filter.limit })
      .map(toEvent);
  }

  /** Spec §6.8 suppression input: the newest **sent** event for the key within [since, now]. A failed send does not suppress. */
  lastFiring(ruleId: string, key: { dedupKey: string } | { identityKey: string }, since: IsoDateTime): AlertEvent | null {
    assertIsoDateTime('since', since);
    const byDedup = 'dedupKey' in key;
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, EventDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM alert_events WHERE rule_id = @ruleId AND ${byDedup ? 'dedup_key' : 'identity_key'} = @key AND status = 'sent' AND fired_at >= @since
         ORDER BY fired_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ ruleId, key: byDedup ? key.dedupKey : key.identityKey, since });
    return r === undefined ? null : toEvent(r);
  }

  /** Spec §5.3: every URL in `payload_json` has its query string and userinfo stripped before storage. */
  insertEvent(e: AlertEventInput): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('fired_at', e.firedAt);
    return Number(
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO alert_events (rule_id, identity_key, observation_id, calendar_price_id, dedup_key, fired_at, status, reason, payload_json)
           VALUES (@ruleId, @identityKey, @observationId, @calendarPriceId, @dedupKey, @firedAt, @status, @reason, @payload)`,
        )
        .run({ ...e, reason: e.reason === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(e.reason), payload: stripUrlsInText(JSON.stringify(e.payload)) }).lastInsertRowid,
    );
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): AlertRule {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], RuleDbRow>('SELECT * FROM alert_rules WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    if (r === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `alert rule ${id} does not exist`);
    return toRule(r);
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/notifications.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import type { OperatorNotificationKind } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7 `operator_notifications`; spec §8.1 "deduplicated per execution and kind". */
export class NotificationsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  wasSent(kind: OperatorNotificationKind, executionId: string | null): boolean {
    return (
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(`SELECT 1 AS present FROM operator_notifications WHERE kind = @kind AND execution_id IS @executionId AND status = 'sent' LIMIT 1`)
        .get({ kind, executionId }) !== undefined
    );
  }

  record(kind: OperatorNotificationKind, executionId: string | null, status: 'sent' | 'failed'): void {
    this.db.raw
      .prepare('INSERT INTO operator_notifications (kind, execution_id, sent_at, status) VALUES (@kind, @executionId, @sentAt, @status)')
      .run({ kind, executionId, sentAt: this.clock.now(), status });
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/routePriceHistory.ts`:

```ts
import type { Cabin, Clock, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, ProviderId } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { assertInt, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { HistoryPoint, RoutePricePoint } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7 `route_price_history`, upserted on every verification. */
export class RoutePriceHistoryRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); `fetchedAt` comes from the response.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  upsert(rows: readonly RoutePricePoint[]): void {
    for (const r of rows) {
      assertInt('lowest_price_pence', r.lowestPricePence);
      assertIsoDate('date', r.date);
      assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', r.fetchedAt);
    }
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO route_price_history (origin, destination, cabin, provider_id, date, lowest_price_pence, fetched_at)
       VALUES (@origin, @destination, @cabin, @providerId, @date, @lowestPricePence, @fetchedAt)
       ON CONFLICT(origin, destination, cabin, provider_id, date) DO UPDATE SET lowest_price_pence = excluded.lowest_price_pence, fetched_at = excluded.fetched_at`,
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) stmt.run(r);
    });
  }

  series(origin: Iata, destination: Iata, cabin: Cabin, providerId: ProviderId): HistoryPoint[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { date: string; lowest_price_pence: number; fetched_at: string }>(
        'SELECT date, lowest_price_pence, fetched_at FROM route_price_history WHERE origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND cabin = @cabin AND provider_id = @providerId ORDER BY date',
      )
      .all({ origin, destination, cabin, providerId })
      .map((r) => ({ fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime, date: r.date as IsoDate, pricePence: r.lowest_price_pence, providerId, sourceKind: null, cabin, seller: null, executionId: null }));
  }
}
```

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteResponseCache } from '../src/responseCache.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';

describe('SqliteResponseCache (spec §5.3 "Cache", §7 provider_cache)', () => {
  it('returns the body with its original storedAt until the TTL passes, then nothing', () => {
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const cache = new SqliteResponseCache(freshDb(), clock);
    const storedAt = '2026-10-14T11:59:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime;
    cache.set('k1', { data: [1, 2] }, 3600, storedAt);
    expect(cache.get('k1')).toEqual({ body: { data: [1, 2] }, storedAt });
    clock.set('2026-10-14T12:58:59.999Z' as IsoDateTime);
    expect(cache.get('k1')).toBeDefined();
    clock.set('2026-10-14T12:59:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime); // storedAt + 3600 s: expired
    expect(cache.get('k1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(cache.get('never-set')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('set replaces an existing key', () => {
    const cache = new SqliteResponseCache(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    cache.set('k', { v: 1 }, 60, T0);
    cache.set('k', { v: 2 }, 60, T0);
    expect(cache.get('k')?.body).toEqual({ v: 2 });
  });

  it('rejects a non-positive or non-integer TTL and a non-ISO storedAt', () => {
    const cache = new SqliteResponseCache(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => cache.set('k', {}, 0, T0)).toThrow(/ttl/);
    expect(() => cache.set('k', {}, 1.5, T0)).toThrow(/ttl/);
    expect(() => cache.set('k', {}, 60, '2026-10-14' as IsoDateTime)).toThrow(/toISOString/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check, index, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isIsoTimestamp } from './checks.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `provider_cache`. `provider_id` is nullable: the `ResponseCache` port's `set` carries no
 * provider (reported mismatch); http-kit's keys are credential-free sha256 hex (spec §5.3).
 */
export const providerCache = sqliteTable(
  'provider_cache',
  {
    key: text('key').primaryKey(),
    providerId: text('provider_id'),
    bodyJson: text('body_json').notNull(),
    storedAt: text('stored_at').notNull(),
    expiresAt: text('expires_at').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [index('provider_cache_expires_idx').on(t.expiresAt), check('provider_cache_stored_ck', isIsoTimestamp('stored_at')), check('provider_cache_expires_ck', isIsoTimestamp('expires_at'))],
);
```

`packages/db/src/responseCache.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime, ResponseCache } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDateTime } from './internal/validate.js';

/**
 * Spec §5.3 response cache over `provider_cache`. A hit returns the original `storedAt`, which
 * http-kit reports as `fetchedAt` (spec §2 "the original storedAt on a cache hit"). Bodies arrive
 * already scrubbed of credentials (http-kit, WP4); this class stores what it is given.
 */
export class SqliteResponseCache implements ResponseCache {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  get(key: string): { body: unknown; storedAt: IsoDateTime } | undefined {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string, string], { body_json: string; stored_at: string }>('SELECT body_json, stored_at FROM provider_cache WHERE key = ? AND expires_at > ?')
      .get(key, this.clock.now());
    return r === undefined ? undefined : { body: JSON.parse(r.body_json) as unknown, storedAt: r.stored_at as IsoDateTime };
  }

  set(key: string, body: unknown, ttlSeconds: number, storedAt: IsoDateTime): void {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(ttlSeconds) || ttlSeconds <= 0) throw new RowRejected('ttl', `ttlSeconds must be a positive integer, got ${ttlSeconds}`);
    assertIsoDateTime('stored_at', storedAt);
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.parse(storedAt) + ttlSeconds * 1000).toISOString();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO provider_cache (key, provider_id, body_json, stored_at, expires_at) VALUES (@key, NULL, @body, @storedAt, @expiresAt)
         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET body_json = excluded.body_json, stored_at = excluded.stored_at, expires_at = excluded.expires_at`,
      )
      .run({ key, body: JSON.stringify(body), storedAt, expiresAt });
  }
}
```

### Task 3.18: `AppLock`: symmetric holders, heartbeat age, stale takeover

**Files:**
- Create: `src/schema/appLock.ts`, `src/appLock.ts`, `test/appLock.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/schema/index.ts`; regenerate the migration.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `AppLock`, `HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS`, `LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS`.

Semantics (spec §7 `app_lock`): liveness is the heartbeat age, never a pid. A lock is **stale** when its heartbeat is older than 3 missed beats of 30 s, i.e. age **> 90,000 ms**. `acquire({ waitMs })` takes the lock if it is absent, stale, or already ours; otherwise it polls until it becomes free or stale, or until `waitMs` has elapsed, then refuses. Callers pick `waitMs` (WP8: the app waits 60 s for a fresh `tool` lock and 90 s for a fresh `app` lock; the tool bootstrap passes 0 and refuses at once). A fresh `app` lock left by an ungraceful stop stops beating, so it turns stale within 90 s of its last beat and the waiting app takes it over, logging the lock age. Every acquisition and takeover runs in `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
import { fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { AppLock, HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS, LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS } from '../src/appLock.js';
import { openDatabase } from '../src/client.js';
import { T0, freshDb, silentLog, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';

/** A sleep that advances the fake clock instead of blocking, so waits are deterministic and instant. */
const sleeper = (clock: ReturnType<typeof fixedClock>) => (ms: number) => clock.advance(ms);

describe('AppLock (spec §7 app_lock)', () => {
  it('constants: beat every 30 s, stale after 3 missed beats', () => {
    expect(HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS).toBe(30_000);
    expect(LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS).toBe(3 * HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS);
  });

  it('symmetry: whichever holder has a fresh lock, the other is refused; release hands it over', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const appDb = freshDb(path);
    const toolDb = openDatabase(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const app = new AppLock(appDb, 'app', clock, silentLog, { sleep: sleeper(clock) });
    const tool = new AppLock(toolDb, 'tool', clock, silentLog, { sleep: sleeper(clock) });
    expect(app.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
    expect(tool.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('refused');
    expect(AppLock.inspect(toolDb)).toEqual({ holder: 'app', heartbeatAt: T0, acquiredAt: T0 });
    app.release();
    expect(AppLock.inspect(toolDb)).toBeNull();
    expect(tool.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
    expect(app.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('refused');
    tool.release();
    toolDb.close();
    appDb.close();
  });

  it('the app waits up to waitMs for a fresh tool lock that keeps beating, then refuses', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const tool = new AppLock(db, 'tool', clock, silentLog);
    expect(tool.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
    const app = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog, {
      pollMs: 5000,
      sleep: (ms) => {
        clock.advance(ms);
        tool.heartbeat(); // the tool is alive
      },
    });
    expect(app.acquire({ waitMs: 60_000 })).toBe('refused');
    expect(clock.now()).toBe('2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z'); // waited the full 60 s
    expect(AppLock.inspect(db)?.holder).toBe('tool');
  });

  it('stale takeover by heartbeat age: 90,000 ms is still fresh, 90,001 ms is stale; the age is logged', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog).acquire({ waitMs: 0 }); // the previous process, never beats again
    const warn = vi.fn();
    const next = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, { ...silentLog, warn }, { pollMs: 1, sleep: sleeper(clock) });
    clock.advance(LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS);
    expect(next.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('refused'); // exactly 90 s: fresh
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(next.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired'); // 90.001 s: stale
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ previousHolder: 'app', lockAgeMs: 90_001 }), expect.stringContaining('stale'));
    expect(AppLock.inspect(db)).toEqual({ holder: 'app', heartbeatAt: '2026-10-14T12:01:30.001Z', acquiredAt: '2026-10-14T12:01:30.001Z' });
  });

  it('a waiting app takes over a crashed app lock within the 90 s wait (spec §7)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog).acquire({ waitMs: 0 });
    clock.advance(30_000); // reboot 30 s after the last beat
    const next = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog, { pollMs: 5000, sleep: sleeper(clock) });
    expect(next.acquire({ waitMs: 90_000 })).toBe('acquired');
    expect(clock.now()).toBe('2026-10-14T12:01:35.000Z'); // first poll past 90 s of heartbeat age
  });

  it('heartbeat refreshes the holder and fails loudly once the lock has been taken over; release never deletes another holder', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const old = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog);
    old.acquire({ waitMs: 0 });
    clock.advance(10_000);
    old.heartbeat();
    expect(AppLock.inspect(db)?.heartbeatAt).toBe('2026-10-14T12:00:10.000Z');
    clock.advance(100_000);
    const next = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog);
    expect(next.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
    expect(() => old.heartbeat()).toThrow(/lost/);
    old.release(); // no-op: not ours any more
    expect(AppLock.inspect(db)?.acquiredAt).toBe('2026-10-14T12:01:50.000Z');
  });

  it('re-acquiring a lock already held by this instance succeeds', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const lock = new AppLock(db, 'app', clock, silentLog);
    expect(lock.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
    expect(lock.acquire({ waitMs: 0 })).toBe('acquired');
  });

  it('only one row can exist (id = 1)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    expect(() => db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO app_lock (id, holder, heartbeat_at, acquired_at) VALUES (2, 'app', ?, ?)`).run(T0, T0)).toThrow(/CHECK/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isOneOf } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `app_lock`: one row (id = 1); liveness judged by heartbeat age. */
export const appLock = sqliteTable(
  'app_lock',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey(),
    holder: text('holder').notNull(),
    heartbeatAt: text('heartbeat_at').notNull(),
    acquiredAt: text('acquired_at').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('app_lock_singleton_ck', sql.raw('id = 1')),
    check('app_lock_holder_ck', isOneOf('holder', ['app', 'tool'])),
    check('app_lock_heartbeat_ck', isIsoTimestamp('heartbeat_at')),
    check('app_lock_acquired_ck', isIsoTimestamp('acquired_at')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/appLock.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime, Logger } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from './client.js';

/** Spec §7: "Heartbeat every 30 s; stale after 3 missed beats". */
export const HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000;
export const LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS = 3 * HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS;

type Holder = 'app' | 'tool';
interface LockRow { holder: Holder; heartbeat_at: string; acquired_at: string }

/** Synchronous sleep for the boot-time wait (nothing else runs while the process is acquiring). */
function blockingSleep(ms: number): void {
  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
}

/**
 * The single-writer lock between the app and the tool bootstrap (spec §7 `app_lock`). Symmetric:
 * either holder with a fresh heartbeat excludes the other. Identity of a holding instance is
 * (holder, acquired_at), so a heartbeat or release from an instance whose lock was taken over is
 * detected rather than applied.
 */
export class AppLock {
  private acquiredAt: IsoDateTime | null = null;
  private readonly sleep: (ms: number) => void;
  private readonly pollMs: number;

  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly holder: Holder,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly log: Logger,
    opts: { sleep?: (ms: number) => void; pollMs?: number } = {},
  ) {
    this.sleep = opts.sleep ?? blockingSleep;
    this.pollMs = opts.pollMs ?? 1000;
  }

  static inspect(db: Db): { holder: Holder; heartbeatAt: IsoDateTime; acquiredAt: IsoDateTime } | null {
    const r = db.raw.prepare<[], LockRow>('SELECT holder, heartbeat_at, acquired_at FROM app_lock WHERE id = 1').get();
    return r === undefined ? null : { holder: r.holder, heartbeatAt: r.heartbeat_at as IsoDateTime, acquiredAt: r.acquired_at as IsoDateTime };
  }

  acquire(opts: { waitMs: number }): 'acquired' | 'refused' {
    const deadline = Date.parse(this.clock.now()) + Math.max(0, opts.waitMs);
    for (;;) {
      if (this.tryAcquire()) return 'acquired';
      const now = Date.parse(this.clock.now());
      if (now >= deadline) {
        const current = AppLock.inspect(this.db);
        this.log.warn({ holder: this.holder, currentHolder: current?.holder, lockAgeMs: current ? now - Date.parse(current.heartbeatAt) : null }, 'app_lock held by a live process; refusing');
        return 'refused';
      }
      this.sleep(Math.min(this.pollMs, deadline - now));
    }
  }

  /** Called every HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS by the bootstrap (WP8). Throws when another process has taken the lock. */
  heartbeat(): void {
    if (this.acquiredAt === null) throw new Error('app_lock heartbeat without holding the lock');
    const changes = this.db.raw
      .prepare('UPDATE app_lock SET heartbeat_at = @now WHERE id = 1 AND holder = @holder AND acquired_at = @acquiredAt')
      .run({ now: this.clock.now(), holder: this.holder, acquiredAt: this.acquiredAt }).changes;
    if (changes !== 1) {
      this.acquiredAt = null;
      throw new Error('app_lock lost: another process took it over');
    }
  }

  /** Spec §7 "Graceful SIGTERM releases the lock after the checkpoint". Never deletes another holder's lock. */
  release(): void {
    if (this.acquiredAt === null) return;
    this.db.raw.prepare('DELETE FROM app_lock WHERE id = 1 AND holder = @holder AND acquired_at = @acquiredAt').run({ holder: this.holder, acquiredAt: this.acquiredAt });
    this.acquiredAt = null;
  }

  /** One attempt under BEGIN IMMEDIATE: take an absent or stale lock, or confirm our own. */
  private tryAcquire(): boolean {
    const attempt = this.db.raw.transaction((): boolean => {
      const now = this.clock.now();
      const row = this.db.raw.prepare<[], LockRow>('SELECT holder, heartbeat_at, acquired_at FROM app_lock WHERE id = 1').get();
      if (row !== undefined && row.holder === this.holder && row.acquired_at === this.acquiredAt) return true;
      if (row !== undefined) {
        const ageMs = Date.parse(now) - Date.parse(row.heartbeat_at);
        if (ageMs <= LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS) return false;
        this.log.warn({ holder: this.holder, previousHolder: row.holder, lockAgeMs: ageMs }, 'taking over a stale app_lock (heartbeat older than 3 missed beats)');
      }
      this.db.raw
        .prepare('INSERT INTO app_lock (id, holder, heartbeat_at, acquired_at) VALUES (1, @holder, @now, @now) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET holder = excluded.holder, heartbeat_at = excluded.heartbeat_at, acquired_at = excluded.acquired_at')
        .run({ holder: this.holder, now });
      this.acquiredAt = now;
      return true;
    });
    return attempt.immediate();
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/maintenance.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime, Logger } from '@gmt/core';
import type { LedgerProviderId } from './budgetConfig.js';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { LedgerRepo } from './repositories/ledger.js';

/** Spec §7: `cache_hit` ledger rows older than this are pruned for monthly-window providers. */
const CACHE_HIT_RETENTION_MS = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/**
 * Spec §7 "Retention", run daily by the engine (WP7): delete expired `provider_cache` rows, prune
 * old `cache_hit` rows for providers on monthly windows only (lifetime-window plans keep every row,
 * since their cap needs them), and log the database size.
 */
export function runMaintenance(db: Db, clock: Clock, log: Logger, opts: { lifetimeProviders: readonly LedgerProviderId[] }): { cacheRowsDeleted: number; ledgerRowsPruned: number; dbBytes: number } {
  const now = clock.now();
  const cacheRowsDeleted = db.raw.prepare('DELETE FROM provider_cache WHERE expires_at <= ?').run(now).changes;
  const lifetime = new Set(opts.lifetimeProviders);
  const monthly = db.raw
    .prepare<[], { p: LedgerProviderId }>(`SELECT DISTINCT provider_id AS p FROM provider_requests WHERE outcome = 'cache_hit'`)
    .all()
    .map((r) => r.p)
    .filter((p) => !lifetime.has(p));
  const cutoff = new Date(Date.parse(now) - CACHE_HIT_RETENTION_MS).toISOString() as IsoDateTime;
  const ledgerRowsPruned = new LedgerRepo(db, clock).pruneCacheHits(cutoff, monthly);
  const pageCount = Number(db.raw.pragma('page_count', { simple: true }));
  const pageSize = Number(db.raw.pragma('page_size', { simple: true }));
  const dbBytes = pageCount * pageSize;
  log.info({ dbBytes, cacheRowsDeleted, ledgerRowsPruned }, 'maintenance');
  return { cacheRowsDeleted, ledgerRowsPruned, dbBytes };
}
```

```ts
// Barrel for @gmt/db. Every public export of the package is re-exported from here; consumers never deep-import.
export * from './client.js';
export * from './migrate.js';
export * from './paths.js';
export * from './types.js';

export * from './budgetGuard.js';
export * from './responseCache.js';
export * from './appLock.js';
export * from './maintenance.js';
export * from './seed/airports.js';
export { AirportsRepo } from './repositories/airports.js';
export { FxRepo } from './repositories/fx.js';
export { DefinitionsRepo } from './repositories/definitions.js';
export { CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, ExecutionsRepo } from './repositories/executions.js';
export { CellsRepo } from './repositories/cells.js';
export { CalendarPricesRepo } from './repositories/calendarPrices.js';
export { ItinerariesRepo } from './repositories/itineraries.js';
export { LedgerRepo } from './repositories/ledger.js';
```

## 9. Pinned review-focus tests

**Plan Review Focus line 5** ("SQLite is busy or the disk is full during a tile write"; WP3 owns the `busy_timeout` assertion and the error's shape; WP7 owns the `paused_error` transition):

- `busy_timeout=5000` asserted on a real file database and on a second handle to it: Task 3.5, `test/migrations.test.ts` › "busy_timeout=5000, WAL and foreign keys hold on a real file". The companion test there shows a blocked write surfaces `SqliteError` with code `SQLITE_BUSY` rather than hanging.
- A repository write inside `db.transaction` that throws `SqliteError` propagates SQLite's message with no URL in it, rolls everything back, and is stored URL-free in `error_text`: Task 3.11, `test/calendarPrices.test.ts` › "plan Review Focus 5: a SqliteError inside a repository transaction".

## 11. Open questions

1. **Metro codes for the airports table** — `requires_human_judgment`; **does not block dispatch.** Spec §7 says `metro` is filled "when the dataset provides one", and spec §2/§5.2 rely on it for Travelpayouts metro tiling (14 tiles instead of 28 in the worked example). mwgg/Airports has **no** metro field, so the vendored dataset ships with `metro = NULL` everywhere, and `sharedMetro` returns `null`, which the spec already handles (Travelpayouts tiles per airport). The verified alternative source is Travelpayouts' own data file `https://api.travelpayouts.com/data/en/airports.json` (no token; each airport carries `city_code`, e.g. LGW → LON, EWR → NYC; 10,378 rows on 2026-10-14). `scripts/build-airports.mjs --metro <file>` merges it at build time (never at seed). Ian to decide whether to vendor that file's `city_code` mapping (its terms are Travelpayouts' affiliate data terms, not MIT) or to accept per-airport tiling.

**Mismatches found while writing this brief** (reported, not fixed; the brief follows the spec where the two disagree):

- Spec §7 `alert_rules.cooldown_hours … default 168` contradicts §6.8 `cooldownHours (default 336, deliberately not equal to the weekly cadence)`. The brief uses 336 (Task 3.16).
- Spec §5.6 says FX rates come "via plain `fetch` + `p-retry` (host-allowlisted, not ledgered)"; §4 and §5.3 say FX goes through http-kit under the never-billable id `fx`, which ledgers it. The brief accepts `fx` ledger rows (never billable, uncapped) so either reading works.
- Spec §5.1 `BudgetGuard.reserve`/`recordCacheHit` have no `tileIndex`, but §5.3 requires `tile_index` on every ledger row. The brief adds an optional `tileIndex` to the implementation; the core port needs it too (WP0/WP4).
- Spec §7 `search_definitions` has no column for `SearchDefinition.destinationNotes` (§11 per-destination note). The brief adds `destination_notes_json`.
- Spec §7 `search_cells` stores only `discovery_status`, `discovery_min_pence`, `verification_status`, while `RollUp` carries `{ status, reason, minPence }` per stage. The brief adds `discovery_reason`, `verification_reason`, `verification_min_pence`.
- Spec §7 `provider_cache.provider_id`: the `ResponseCache.set` port carries no provider, so the column is nullable and written NULL.
- Plan "Database" block names types it never defines (`Airport`, `AirportsDataset`, `Execution`, `TileWrite`, `VerifiedCellWrite`, `CalendarPriceRow`, `ObservationRow`, `HistoryPoint`, `AlertRule`, `AlertEvent`, `BudgetConfig`); the brief defines them in `packages/db/src/types.ts` (section 5).
- Plan: `applyMigrations` returns `{ applied: string[] }` in the contract but `void` in WP0 Task 0.10; Task 3.5 brings it to the contract. `Db.transaction`'s contract type differs slightly from WP0's implementation type.
- Plan: WP1's `validateSearchSpace` lookup needs `airportsOfMetro`, which `AirportsRepo` in the contract lacks; added.
- Plan: `CellsRepo.buckets` returns `Buckets`, but WP0's api-contract defines only `bucketsSchema`, and the self-review places it in `common.ts` while the contract list places it in `results.ts`. The brief imports `type Buckets` and `type Estimate` from `@gmt/core`; WP0 must export both `z.infer` types from its barrel.
- Plan: repositories are `new XRepo(db, clock)`, but spec §5.5 requires repositories to reject synthetic rows when `syntheticAllowed` is false, which needs the gate inside them. The brief adds an optional third argument `{ syntheticAllowed }` defaulting to `false`.
- Plan: WP3 depends only on WP0, yet stored roll-ups need the §6.6 table that WP1's `rollUp` implements; the brief computes it in SQL, and WP7 should add a parity test against `core.rollUp`.
